【助理／進度訊息】
圖 2 的 `clock_reset : True` 是關鍵：檢查程式偵測到 Stop 後模擬時間倒退，便停止累計影像，所以組數停在 151。我會對照目前程式確認這個流程，再解讀第一次 Play 的有效量測。

【助理】
**151 不再增加，是檢查程式偵測到時間重設後，刻意停止累計。**

Stop 後重新 Play，模擬時間會從較小值重新開始。[程式這段](/home/noel_614420090/reconview/ros2_ws/dataset_node/check/tarnsport.py:269) 會設定 `clock_reset=True`，之後每次收到影像便提早結束處理，避免把兩次模擬的資料混在一起。Pause 不會解除這個狀態，必須重啟檢查程式。

因此圖 2、3 的組數 **151** 和三路缺口 **0** 都是停止累計前的值；CPU、GPU 則仍持續取樣。`publishers_ready=True` 只表示發布端仍存在。

圖 1 的有效結果可以先這樣讀：

- **125 → 126 → 128**：完整影像組持續收到。
- **三路缺口都是 0**：收到的前後訊息之間，尚未發現發布序號缺口；開頭、結尾遺失仍未涵蓋。
- **模擬速度約 0.43**：實際過 1 秒，模擬只前進約 0.43 秒。原本每模擬秒最多 5 組，換算約為每實際秒最多 **2.2 組**。

目前看到的是「模擬較慢，這段尚未發現傳輸缺口」。CPU 最忙的邏輯核心曾達 100%，是後續追查線索，還不能直接認定原因。

現在保持 **Pause**，在檢查程式終端機按 Ctrl+C，再執行：

```bash
/usr/bin/python3 /home/noel_614420090/reconview/ros2_ws/dataset_node/check/tarnsport.py
```

接著按 Play 跑約 20 秒，複製一個執行中的摘要，再按 Pause。新的完整組數應重新從 0 開始增加。

## My request:
但是一旦 stop 就會停留在固定的 group_total，這會有影響嗎? 
我們要檢視的是硬體是否會影響傳輸


【助理】
**會影響 Stop 之後的量測有效性。** 檢查程式停止累計，但沒有停止 Isaac Sim 的影像發布。因此停在 151、缺口維持 0，都只是舊結果，不能用來判斷第二次 Play 的傳輸。

你確認重啟檢查程式後組數會重新增加，代表量測可以恢復。:codex-annotation{index="1"}

我們要驗證的是：**硬體負載改變時，已發布的影像是否更容易在接收路徑遺失。** `group_total` 只是收到多少組，單看它增加快慢不夠：

| 觀察結果 | 能判讀的事情 |
|---|---|
| 模擬變慢，發布序號缺口仍為 0 | 目前觀測區間未發現傳輸缺失 |
| 發布序號出現缺口 | 有已發布訊息未到達檢查端，仍需查明原因 |

圖 1 已提供初步基準：模擬速度約 **0.43**、三路序號缺口 **0**。CPU 曾滿載，但這還不能證明 CPU 造成傳輸掉幀。

接下來應做**相同場景、解析度、發布頻率與 QoS，只改 CPU 負載的兩輪對照**。每輪使用新的檢查統計，在連續 Play 期間量測。Stop 視為結束該輪，重新 Play 就開始新一輪；這樣才能把硬體負載與傳輸結果對應起來。

【使用者】
1. 所以需要再寫新的檔案?&#x20;
2. 帶我理解/home/noel_614420090/reconview/ros2_ws/dataset_node/check/tarnsport.py的輸入輸出，以及你寫了很多的函數我需要理解嗎? 這裡只記錄不做判斷? 


【助理／進度訊息】
不需要再新增檔案，目前這支就能做量測與兩輪對照。我會用唯讀方式對照程式，先說明輸入、輸出，以及它實際做了哪些檢查，函數則先聚焦核心流程。

【助理】
**不需要新增檔案。** 目前這支就能量測，再用不同負載的兩輪結果做對照。

這支 [tarnsport.py](/home/noel_614420090/reconview/ros2_ws/dataset_node/check/tarnsport.py) 包含不少輔助診斷，對目前階段確實偏大。你需要理解核心資料怎麼進來、數字怎麼算、哪些結果不能直接下結論；不用一次讀懂所有函數與 Python 語法。

它的**輸入**有四類：

| 輸入 | 內容 | 用途 |
|---|---|---|
| 三路 ROS 2 `Image` | 前視 RGB、前視 depth、俯視 RGB | 累計收到的影像，檢查三路是否能配成一組 |
| `/clock` | Isaac Sim 模擬時間 | 計算模擬速度、偵測時間倒退 |
| DDS 訊息資訊與 ROS graph | 發布序號、發布端身分及配對事件 | 比較序號缺口，避免混用不同發布端 |
| 本機硬體資訊 | `/proc` 與 `nvidia-smi` | 讀取 CPU、RAM、Swap、GPU 負載 |

影像資料目前來自這三個 topic：

```text
/fpv_camera/rgb
/fpv_camera/depth
/top_camera/rgb
```

RGB 格式為 `rgb8`，depth 為 `32FC1`、單位是公尺。程式會接收像素資料並檢查格式，但沒有分析影像內容。它使用 `header.stamp` 的擷取時間來配組，**不會把畫面內容相似當成同一組**。

硬體數值是**整台主機**的負載，包含其他程式；例如 CPU 某個邏輯核心達到 100%，還不能直接認定是 Isaac Sim 使用的。

它的**輸出**是每秒一次的終端機摘要：

- `complete_groups_total`：三路影像具有相同擷取時間戳記，才增加一組。
- 各路 `seq_missing_total`：前後收到的發布序號之間，缺少幾筆訊息。
- `sim_wall_ratio_est`：模擬前進時間 ÷ 實際經過時間。
- CPU、RAM、GPU 等資源數值。

目前只把統計保留在記憶體，**沒有自動保存 CSV、影像或實驗紀錄**。

至於「只記錄、不做判斷」，更精確地說：**它有做局部檢查與計算，但沒有自動判定硬體造成掉幀。**

例如收到的發布序號為：

```text
100 → 101 → 104
```

程式會算出中間缺少 `102、103`，累計缺口增加 **2**。這是依據序號做的判斷；但它無法單靠這件事判定原因是 CPU、DDS 佇列或網路。開頭第一筆之前、最後一筆之後的遺失，也不在這個比較範圍內。

目前先理解這四個核心函數即可：

| 函數 | 負責的事情 |
|---|---|
| `_on_image()` | 每收到一張影像，更新統計、檢查格式、嘗試配組 |
| `observe_sequence()` | 比較前後發布序號，累計缺口 |
| `_on_clock()` | 更新模擬時間；時間倒退時停止該輪影像量測 |
| `report()` | 計算這一秒的統計，印出摘要 |

它們由事件觸發：**影像抵達就呼叫 `_on_image()`，`/clock` 抵達就呼叫 `_on_clock()`，每秒呼叫一次 `report()`。** `main()` 裡的 `rclpy.spin(node)` 就是在等待並處理這些事件。

其他函數目前先知道用途即可：初始化負責接線，硬體函數負責讀取資源，關閉函數負責結束背景工作。後續要修改哪一部分，再逐段讀懂對應的實作。

我前面一次交付完整工具，再用函數名稱解釋，跳過了「Python 如何執行程式」這一層，讓你只能依照指令看結果。接下來改成**你親手加一小段、執行、看結果，我再帶你解釋**。

先從一個觀念開始：**定義函數，和呼叫函數，是兩件事。**

在 VS Code 打開 [tarnsport.py](/home/noel_614420090/reconview/ros2_ws/dataset_node/check/tarnsport.py:419)，你會看到：

```python
def main(args=None) -> None:
```

`def` 是「定義」：把下面縮排的程式內容取名為 `main`，等待之後使用。**讀到這行時，不會立刻執行函數裡面的內容。** 括號內與箭頭後面的語法，我們之後再拆開。

接著看檔案最下面：

```python
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

當你用 `python3` 直接執行這個檔案，這個 `if` 條件會成立，接著執行 `main()`。

**`main()` 就是呼叫：現在進入上面定義好的 `main`，開始執行它裡面的程式。**

現在由你做一個小修改：在 `main` 裡面第一行加入 `print`，原本內容保留在後面：

```python
def main(args=None) -> None:
    print("Entered main()", flush=True)
```

這行前面要有 **4 個空白**，表示它屬於 `main` 函數。`print` 會把括號裡的文字印到終端機；`flush=True` 讓文字立即顯示。

儲存後，停止舊的檢查程式，再執行：

```bash
/usr/bin/python3 /home/noel_614420090/reconview/ros2_ws/dataset_node/check/tarnsport.py
```

你應該會看到一次：

```text
Entered main()
```

後面仍會出現原本的統計摘要。這一步先驗證：**檔案底部的 `main()`，確實讓你剛加入函數內的那行程式執行了。** 不需要操作 Play。

貼上你修改後 `main` 開頭的幾行，我先幫你確認縮排與執行位置。

## My request:
模擬跟不上實際時間有何影響?


【助理／進度訊息】
模擬約 0.43 倍速，會讓收集相同模擬時長的資料花更久，也會降低每「實際秒」收到的影像數；這本身不能當成傳輸掉幀。

我會依你選的第一步，先唯讀確認現有耗時欄位與 Isaac Sim 程序，讓你下一次 Play 時能對照相機耗時和執行緒負載。:codex-annotation{index="1"}

【助理】
以目前約 **0.43 倍速**來說，主要有三個影響：

- **資料蒐集變慢**：取得 60 秒模擬資料，約需 140 秒實際時間。
- **每實際秒收到的影像變少**：每模擬秒最多 5 組，換算約每實際秒最多 2.15 組，不能因此判成掉幀。
- **控制與 timeout 要確認時間基準**：若某個節點依實際時間計時，其他部分依模擬時間運作，可能改變控制頻率、等待期限及時間對齊。

慢速本身不代表資料無效。資料與控制是否正確，要看時間戳記和同步方式；也不能用慢速模擬結果直接宣稱系統能即時運作。

現在帶你做選定的第一步：**找出 CPU 忙碌是否主要來自相機處理。** 使用現有輸出與系統工具即可。:codex-annotation{index="1"}

請在**同一次連續 Play** 期間取得以下兩份資訊：

1. 按 Play，等待約 10 秒。
2. 到啟動 Isaac Sim 的終端機，複製一個 `[sim_node]` 摘要，包含 `camera_callback_thread_id`、`camera_work_cpu_percent` 與四個 `*_cpu_ms`。
3. 在另一個 VS Code 終端機執行：

```bash
top -H -p 1830701
```

我剛確認目前 Isaac Sim 的 PID 是 `1830701`。如果你已重啟 Isaac Sim，先用 `pgrep -x kit` 查新的 PID。

在 `top` 按大寫 **P**，依 CPU 使用率排序，等畫面更新兩三次，再複製或截取前幾列。完成後可按 Pause；`top` 按 `q` 離開。

先記住兩個讀法：

- `top -H` 的 `PID` 欄代表**執行緒 ID**，可對照 `camera_callback_thread_id`。
- `read_cpu_ms` 等數字是**該統計視窗內累計的 CPU 時間**，不是單張影像耗時。

貼上這兩份結果，我們就能先區分「相機程式消耗較多 CPU」與「Isaac Sim 其他工作消耗較多 CPU」。

## My request:
帶我看懂

[圖片附件：原始路徑見本則訊息的 Files mentioned by the user 區塊。]

【助理／進度訊息】
這份摘要已能看出：在約 1.021 秒的統計期間，相機流程用了約 23% 的單一執行緒 CPU 時間，其中大部分花在讀取影像。我會對照目前程式，說明「執行了 28 次、發布 1 組」的意義，以及哪些數字還不能當成掉幀證據。

【助理】
這份結果先提供一個線索：**這段相機程式的 CPU 成本，主要花在讀取影像；ROS 發布呼叫本身的 CPU 成本很小。** 但還不能據此判定整個系統的瓶頸。

先把這份報告想成「**約 1 秒內的工作帳單**」。

`profile_window_wall_s : 1.021` 表示下面的統計涵蓋 **1.021 秒實際時間**，不是模擬時間。

這段期間，四個階段累計使用的 CPU 時間為：

| 欄位 | 做了什麼 | 累計 CPU 時間 |
|---|---|---:|
| `read_cpu_ms` | 讀取相機快照、複製資料、檢查格式 | **221.819 ms** |
| `resize_cpu_ms` | 將影像縮成發布尺寸 | **12.465 ms** |
| `pack_cpu_ms` | 將像素打包成 ROS `Image` | **0.561 ms** |
| `publish_call_cpu_ms` | 呼叫 ROS 發布函數 | **1.009 ms** |

**這些是整個統計期間的累計值，不是單張影像耗時。** 四項合計約 236 ms，其中讀取階段占約 **94%**。

因此：

```text
camera_work_cpu_percent : 23.121
```

可以讀成：在這 1.021 秒內，相機流程使用了約 **236 ms 的執行緒 CPU 時間**，相當於一個邏輯 CPU 約 23.1% 的計算能力。它沒有涵蓋 GPU 或其他執行緒的工作。

再看「執行幾次、發布幾組」：

| 欄位 | 正確解讀 |
|---|---|
| `camera_calls : 28` | 相機處理回呼執行了 28 次，每次會檢查兩個相機 |
| `published_groups_window : 1` | 這段期間完成了 1 組發布呼叫，即前視 RGB、前視 depth、俯視 RGB |
| `published_groups_total : 4642` | 自上次 Stop 重設後，累計完成 4642 組發布呼叫 |

**28 次回呼不等於取得 28 組新影像，也不代表少送了 27 組。** 重複影像會略過，而且只有影像對齊、滿足 200 ms 模擬時間間隔時才發布。

不過，目前程式是**先讀取新影像，再判斷是否需要發布**。所以有些最後沒有發布的新影像，仍已付出讀取、複製的成本。這是值得後續驗證的線索。

另外三個數字：

- `camera_work_wall_max_ms : 39.845`：這 28 次回呼中，最慢的一次實際經過約 **39.8 ms**，包含處理與等待；它不是 ROS 傳輸延遲。
- `latest_stamp_delta_ns : 0`：報告當下，兩個相機最新影像的擷取時間差為 0。
- `same_render_reference : True`：兩者的 render reference 相同，這次對齊檢查正常。

最後，`camera_callback_thread_id : 1830701` 對上你上一張 `top` 的 `kit` 那列。那張圖約 93.4% 是該執行緒的整體 CPU 使用率，這份約 23.1% 只涵蓋相機流程。**兩張不是同一取樣期間，不能精確相減。**

目前已找到「影像讀取／複製成本偏高」這個候選問題；發布呼叫耗時小，也仍不能證明接收端完整收到。下一步值得驗證的是：**減少不會發布的新影像複製，是否能提高模擬速度，同時維持傳輸完整性。**

## 一、專案目標

由使用者逐步建立無人機導航研究系統，涵蓋 Isaac Sim、PX4、ROS 2，以及後續的資料蒐集、模型訓練與 Closed-loop Evaluation。使用者應能解釋、修改與驗證核心流程；可使用官方套件與範例。

## 二、架構與命名決定權

- **專案架構、資料夾結構、檔案名稱與位置，以及模組和套件的劃分，全部由使用者決定。** AI 僅提供選項、理由與影響分析，除非使用者明確委託該次決策。
- 新增、拆分、合併、搬移或重新命名前，先提出具體方案，由使用者決定後再執行；此規則在工程模式下也適用。
- 不在範例或指令中暗自加入未決定的結構與檔名。已決定的項目持續沿用，不重複詢問或擅自調整。
- 工具若有限制或會自動產生檔案，先說明限制，由使用者決定可設定的輸出位置與命名規則，之後依規則執行。
- 可以建議檔案或資料夾名命名以及架構設計。


## 三、工作模式與教學
- 工作模式: 帶我驗證，告知修改內容，代碼解釋。
- **預設採教學模式：使用者編寫與執行，AI 負責說明、提示、審查與協助除錯。** 不自行代寫完整程式、建立專案骨架、覆寫成果或執行開發與測試；已有明確授權的範圍依授權進行。
- 每次聚焦一個小目標：說明必要觀念 → 使用者實作 → 審查與驗證。遇到困難先提示，再給最小範例，不一次提供大量程式碼或待辦。
- 不預設使用者熟悉程式語法。說明設計原因、輸入輸出、主要流程與驗證方式，以實際操作確認理解；概念問題可直接回答，不強制安排練習。
- 「解釋、分析、比較、規劃、檢查」預設唯讀；使用者明確要求直接實作或指定工程模式時，完成最小必要修改與適當驗證，並說明結果。
- 工作模式依每次任務判斷；已授權的必要步驟不重複確認。會影響目標或介面的關鍵歧義，先提出精簡問題。
- 說明使用繁體中文與台灣用語，專有名詞保留英文，例如 ENU、NED、Behavior Cloning；程式碼、識別名稱、註解與 Docstring 一律使用英文。完整提示詞放在獨立、可複製的文字區塊。

## 四、漸進開發

- 一次主要增加一個可驗證的功能，先完成最小流程，再依具體需求增加架構、依賴與自動化。
- 不預先建立完整框架或加入未採用的演算法，不為簡單功能新增不必要的 Node、Wrapper 或抽象層；提議新增檔案或依賴時，說明必要性。
- 舊專案可供對照，不直接複製整套架構；沿用片段前說明用途、相依條件與驗證方式。引用第三方程式須保留適用的授權與來源。
- 文件保留必要的設定、決策與證據，不為每個小改動新增文件，也不要求使用者一次規劃整份專案。

## 五、技術介面與驗證

- 串接元件時，明確定義資料格式、單位、Coordinate Frame、Timestamp 與更新頻率。涉及 ENU、NED、Body Frame 或 World Frame 轉換時，以簡單案例驗證方向與正負號。
- 修改控制命令、模型輸入輸出或時間對齊方式時，檢查上下游相容性。依改動選擇直接相關的驗證；飛控與系統整合不得只憑語法檢查宣稱正確。
- 區分 Windows 本機、Ubuntu Server 與模擬環境的路徑及執行位置，不假設已連到使用者的 Server。版本與 API 以所選版本的官方文件為準，不套用舊專案設定。
- 模擬驗證不等於實機可用；實機操作須有明確任務範圍與相應驗證。

## 六、研究紀錄與操作範圍

- 區分已確認事實、待驗證假說與未知事項，優先用最小測試區分原因。回報時區分已完成、未執行、建議操作與尚待驗證。
- 區分 Training、Validation、Test、Offline Evaluation 與 Closed-loop Evaluation；不以 Training Loss 或 Offline Error 下降宣稱泛化能力或飛行成功。
- 實驗保存必要的 Dataset、Episode、Split、Seed、Hyperparameters、Checkpoint 與程式版本。評估前定義成功、碰撞與超時條件，不事後放寬條件、隱藏失敗或忽略例外錯誤。
- 不修改無關檔案或使用者的未提交變更，不擅自刪除或覆寫舊專案、資料集、模型與實驗紀錄；舊結果不得當作新專案已驗證的成果。
- 大量資料蒐集、昂貴訓練、批次模擬，以及 Git Commit、Push、建立分支或破壞性操作，須在使用者授權範圍內進行。
- 不將密碼、Token 或 SSH Private Key 寫入程式、文件或輸出。

## 七、終端機狀態與統計輸出

- 供人閱讀的週期性狀態與統計，預設使用多行區塊，前後以 `=` 分隔線包住。
- 每行顯示一個欄位，使用 `field_name : value`，固定欄位順序並對齊冒號；相關欄位之間可用空白行分組。
- 欄位名稱使用英文，時間、距離等數值須標示單位，例如 `_s`、`_ms`、`_ns`、`_m`。
- Dictionary 或多路統計應拆成個別欄位，避免在同一行展開大量內容。
- 同一次統計以一次 logger 呼叫輸出完整區塊；週期性摘要預設每秒一次，避免每個 frame 或 physics step 都輸出完整區塊。
- 錯誤保留原有嚴重程度與 traceback；終端機排版不得改變資料內容、時間戳記或統計意義。
- 此規則適用於人閱讀的終端機輸出；機器讀取與研究紀錄依其選定格式保存。

from array import array
import sys
import time

import numpy as np
from PIL import Image as PILImage
from rclpy.qos import qos_profile_sensor_data
from sensor_msgs.msg import Image


class CameraReader: # CameraReader 負責讀取、檢查與辨識新 frame
    """Read new camera snapshots with their source rendering timestamps."""
    
    # 輸入架構參數 CameraReader(sensor, include_depth=True)
    def __init__(self, sensor, *, include_depth: bool = False):
        self.sensor = sensor
        self.include_depth = include_depth
        self.reset()

    def reset(self) -> None:
        """Reset source acquisition timing and discard the previous run's frames."""
        # Pegasus creates this Camera before it exposes it through sensor.state.
        self.sensor._camera.post_reset()
        self.last_frame = None
        self.new_frames = 0
        self.repeated_reads = 0
        self.status = "waiting_for_sensor"
        self.wait_detail = ""

    def read(self):
        """Return a new owned snapshot, or None while waiting or repeating."""
        sensor_state = self.sensor.state
        if not sensor_state or "camera" not in sensor_state:
            self.status = "waiting_for_sensor"
            return None

        camera = sensor_state["camera"]
        current = camera.get_current_frame()
        reference = current.get("rendering_frame")
        if not isinstance(reference, dict):
            self.status = "waiting_for_render"
            self.wait_detail = (
                f"source_ref_type={type(reference).__name__}, "
                f"source_ref={repr(reference)[:80]}, "
                f"source_rendering_time={current.get('rendering_time')!r}"
            )
            return None

        reference_key = (
            int(reference["referenceTimeNumerator"]),
            int(reference["referenceTimeDenominator"]),
        )
        if self.last_frame is not None:
            if reference_key == self.last_frame["render_reference"]:
                self.repeated_reads += 1
                self.status = "repeated_frame"
                return None

        # clone=True 會複製目前 frame，讓我們保留的資料具有獨立內容
        snapshot = camera.get_current_frame(clone=True)
        color = snapshot.get("rgb")
        depth = snapshot.get("distance_to_image_plane")
        if color is None or color.size == 0:
            self.status = "waiting_for_rgb"
            return None
        if self.include_depth and (depth is None or depth.size == 0):
            self.status = "waiting_for_depth"
            return None

        expected_shape = (sensor_state["height"], sensor_state["width"])
        if (
            color.ndim != 3
            or color.shape[:2] != expected_shape
            or color.shape[2] not in (3, 4)
            or color.dtype != np.uint8
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                f"{sensor_state['camera_name']}: unexpected color array "
                f"shape={color.shape}, dtype={color.dtype}"
            )
        rgb = np.ascontiguousarray(color[:, :, :3])

        if self.include_depth:
            if depth.ndim == 3 and depth.shape[2] == 1:
                depth = depth[:, :, 0]
            if depth.shape != expected_shape or depth.dtype != np.float32:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"{sensor_state['camera_name']}: unexpected depth array "
                    f"shape={depth.shape}, dtype={depth.dtype}"
                )

        rendering_time = float(snapshot["rendering_time"])
        if not np.isfinite(rendering_time) or rendering_time < 0:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid rendering time: {rendering_time}")
        stamp_ns = round(rendering_time * 1_000_000_000)
        previous_stamp = None if self.last_frame is None else self.last_frame["stamp_ns"]
        if previous_stamp is not None and stamp_ns <= previous_stamp:
            raise ValueError(
                f"{sensor_state['camera_name']}: capture time did not advance "
                f"({previous_stamp} -> {stamp_ns}); reset reader state before a new run"
            )

        reference = snapshot["rendering_frame"]
        # read() 的輸出
        self.last_frame = {
            "camera_name": sensor_state["camera_name"],
            "camera_prim_path": sensor_state["stage_prim_path"],
            "rendering_time": rendering_time,
            "stamp_ns": stamp_ns,
            "dt_ns": None if previous_stamp is None else stamp_ns - previous_stamp,
            "render_reference": (
                int(reference["referenceTimeNumerator"]),
                int(reference["referenceTimeDenominator"]),
            ),
            "rgb": rgb,
            "depth": depth if self.include_depth else None,
        }
        self.new_frames += 1
        self.status = "new_frame"
        return self.last_frame

    def describe(self) -> str:
        """Describe the latest snapshot without logging full image arrays."""
        if self.last_frame is None or self.status.startswith("waiting_"):
            details = (
                f"status={self.status}, new_frames={self.new_frames}, "
                f"repeated_reads={self.repeated_reads}"
            )
            if self.status == "waiting_for_render":
                details += f", {self.wait_detail}"
            if self.last_frame is not None:
                details += f", last_valid_stamp_ns={self.last_frame['stamp_ns']}"
            return details

        frame = self.last_frame
        rgb = frame["rgb"]
        details = (
            f"status={self.status}, t={frame['rendering_time']:.6f}s, "
            f"stamp_ns={frame['stamp_ns']}, dt_ns={frame['dt_ns']}, "
            f"ref={frame['render_reference']}, rgb={rgb.shape}/{rgb.dtype}, "
            f"new_frames={self.new_frames}, repeated_reads={self.repeated_reads}"
        )
        if frame["depth"] is not None:
            depth = frame["depth"]
            valid = np.isfinite(depth) & (depth > 0)
            details += (
                f", depth={depth.shape}/{depth.dtype}, "
                f"depth_valid_ratio={np.count_nonzero(valid) / depth.size:.3f}"
            )
        return details


class CameraPublisher:
    """Publish optionally downsampled RGB/depth through an existing ROS node."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        node,
        *,
        rgb_topic: str,
        frame_id: str,
        depth_topic=None,
        output_size: tuple[int, int] | None = None,
    ):
        if output_size is not None:
            if len(output_size) != 2 or any(
                type(size) is not int or size <= 0 for size in output_size
            ):
                raise ValueError(
                    "Output size must contain positive integer width and height"
                )
            output_size = tuple(output_size) # 在打包訊息前縮圖
        self.output_size = output_size
        self.frame_id = frame_id
        self.last_profile = {}
        self.rgb_publisher = node.create_publisher(
            Image, rgb_topic, qos_profile_sensor_data
        )
        self.depth_publisher = (
            None
            if depth_topic is None
            else node.create_publisher(Image, depth_topic, qos_profile_sensor_data)
        )

    def _resize_pixels(self, pixels, resample: PILImage.Resampling):
        """Downsample without changing aspect ratio or modifying source arrays."""
        height, width = pixels.shape[:2]
        if self.output_size is None or self.output_size == (width, height):
            return pixels

        output_width, output_height = self.output_size
        if (
            output_width > width
            or output_height > height
            or output_width * height != output_height * width
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                f"Output size {self.output_size} must preserve the aspect ratio "
                f"and not exceed the source size {(width, height)}"
            )

        resized = PILImage.fromarray(pixels).resize(
            self.output_size, resample=resample
        )
        return np.asarray(resized)

    # 填入尺寸、encoding、frame_id、像素資料，並用 divmod() 將擷取時間拆成 sec 與 nanosec。step 是每列資料的 byte 數；data 是打包後的像素。
    def _make_image(self, pixels, encoding: str, stamp_ns: int) -> Image:
        """Serialize output pixels with their source acquisition stamp."""
        height, width = pixels.shape[:2]
        channels = pixels.shape[2] if pixels.ndim == 3 else 1
        msg = Image()
        msg.header.stamp.sec, msg.header.stamp.nanosec = divmod(
            stamp_ns, 1_000_000_000
        )
        msg.header.frame_id = self.frame_id
        msg.height = int(height)
        msg.width = int(width)
        msg.encoding = encoding
        msg.is_bigendian = int(sys.byteorder == "big")
        msg.step = int(width * channels * pixels.dtype.itemsize)
        # C order packs each row; array('B') avoids per-byte Python validation.
        msg.data = array("B", pixels.tobytes(order="C"))
        return msg

    # rgb8 是每個色彩通道 8 bits；32FC1 是每個像素一個 32-bit 浮點數。
    def publish(self, frame) -> None:
        """Publish one new snapshot, preserving a shared RGB/depth timestamp."""
        if self.depth_publisher is not None and frame["depth"] is None:
            raise ValueError("Depth publisher requires a snapshot with depth")

        # Measure only this calling thread; source acquisition stamps stay intact.
        profile = {}
        wall_start_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
        cpu_start_ns = time.thread_time_ns()
        rgb = self._resize_pixels(frame["rgb"], PILImage.Resampling.BOX)
        depth = None
        if self.depth_publisher is not None:
            # Nearest-neighbor sampling keeps source depth values in meters.
            depth = self._resize_pixels(
                frame["depth"], PILImage.Resampling.NEAREST
            )
        profile["resize_cpu_ns"] = time.thread_time_ns() - cpu_start_ns
        profile["resize_wall_ns"] = time.perf_counter_ns() - wall_start_ns

        wall_start_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
        cpu_start_ns = time.thread_time_ns()
        rgb_msg = self._make_image(rgb, "rgb8", frame["stamp_ns"])
        depth_msg = None
        if depth is not None:
            depth_msg = self._make_image(depth, "32FC1", frame["stamp_ns"])
        profile["pack_cpu_ns"] = time.thread_time_ns() - cpu_start_ns
        profile["pack_wall_ns"] = time.perf_counter_ns() - wall_start_ns

        wall_start_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
        cpu_start_ns = time.thread_time_ns()
        self.rgb_publisher.publish(rgb_msg)
        if depth_msg is not None:
            self.depth_publisher.publish(depth_msg)
        # Returning from publish() does not acknowledge subscriber delivery.
        profile["publish_call_cpu_ns"] = time.thread_time_ns() - cpu_start_ns
        profile["publish_call_wall_ns"] = time.perf_counter_ns() - wall_start_ns
        self.last_profile = profile

"""Inspect exact-time image groups before adding dataset recording."""

from collections import deque
from functools import partial
import math

import rclpy
from rclpy.clock import Clock as RclpyClock
from rclpy.clock_type import ClockType
from rclpy.event_handler import SubscriptionEventCallbacks
from rclpy.executors import ExternalShutdownException
from rclpy.node import Node
from rclpy.qos import QoSProfile, ReliabilityPolicy, qos_profile_sensor_data
from rosgraph_msgs.msg import Clock
from sensor_msgs.msg import Image


class DatasetNode(Node):
    """Match three Image streams by acquisition stamp within one simulation run."""

    STREAMS = {
        "fpv_rgb": ("/fpv_camera/rgb", "rgb8", "fpv_camera_optical_frame"),
        "fpv_depth": ("/fpv_camera/depth", "32FC1", "fpv_camera_optical_frame"),
        "top_rgb": ("/top_camera/rgb", "rgb8", "top_camera_optical_frame"),
    }

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__("dataset_node")
        self.declare_parameter("stats_detail", False)
        self._max_pending = self.declare_parameter("max_pending_groups", 10).value
        wait_seconds = self.declare_parameter("max_wait_sim_sec", 1.0).value
        if self._max_pending <= 0:
            raise ValueError("max_pending_groups must be positive")
        if not math.isfinite(wait_seconds) or wait_seconds <= 0:
            raise ValueError("max_wait_sim_sec must be finite and positive")
        self._max_wait_ns = round(wait_seconds * 1_000_000_000)
        if self._max_wait_ns < 1:
            raise ValueError("max_wait_sim_sec must be at least one nanosecond")

        self._sim_time_ns = None
        self._clock_reset = False
        self._pending = {}
        # Remember recent outcomes so a late message cannot reopen a closed group.
        self._closed_stamps = deque(maxlen=max(100, self._max_pending * 4))
        self.latest_group = None
        self._received = dict.fromkeys(self.STREAMS, 0)
        self._dds_lost = dict.fromkeys(self.STREAMS, 0)
        self._missing = dict.fromkeys(self.STREAMS, 0)
        self._complete = 0
        self._incomplete = 0
        self._discard_reasons = {"timeout": 0, "capacity": 0}
        self._duplicates = 0
        self._closed_ignored = 0
        self._invalid = 0
        self._blocked_after_reset = 0
        self._last_age_ns = None
        self._max_age_ns = None

        clock_qos = QoSProfile(depth=1, reliability=ReliabilityPolicy.BEST_EFFORT)
        self._clock_subscription = self.create_subscription(
            Clock, "/clock", self._on_clock, clock_qos
        )
        self._image_subscriptions = []
        for stream, (topic, _encoding, _frame_id) in self.STREAMS.items():
            subscription = self.create_subscription(
                Image,
                topic,
                partial(self._on_image, stream),
                qos_profile_sensor_data,
                event_callbacks=SubscriptionEventCallbacks(
                    message_lost=partial(self._on_message_lost, stream)
                ),
            )
            self._image_subscriptions.append(subscription)

        # Keep reporting during Pause; expiration uses simulation time only.
        self._report_clock = RclpyClock(clock_type=ClockType.STEADY_TIME)
        self._stats_timer = self.create_timer(
            1.0, self._report, clock=self._report_clock
        )
        self.get_logger().info(
            "Inspecting fpv_rgb/fpv_depth/top_rgb with exact acquisition stamps; "
            f"max_pending_groups={self._max_pending}, "
            f"max_wait_sim_sec={wait_seconds}; depth units=m; "
            "last_sim_age_ms estimates group completion age from the latest /clock"
        )
        # last_sim_age_ms = 最後收到的 /clock − 該完整組的影像擷取時間，這是模擬時間差的估計，會受不同 topic 送達順序影響；先量測數值，再判斷延遲狀況。

    # 更新模擬時間；若時間倒退，清空暫存並停止配對，要求重啟接收端。
    def _on_clock(self, msg: Clock) -> None:
        """Track simulation time and stop pairing if its timeline moves backwards."""
        now_ns = msg.clock.sec * 1_000_000_000 + msg.clock.nanosec
        if (
            not self._clock_reset
            and self._sim_time_ns is not None
            and now_ns < self._sim_time_ns
        ):
            discarded = len(self._pending)
            self._pending.clear()
            self._closed_stamps.clear()
            self.latest_group = None
            self._last_age_ns = None
            self._clock_reset = True
            self.get_logger().error(
                "Simulation time moved backwards; pairing stopped and "
                f"{discarded} pending groups cleared. "
                "Restart dataset_node for a new simulation run."
            )
        self._sim_time_ns = now_ns
        for group in self._pending.values():
            if group["first_seen_sim_ns"] is None:
                group["first_seen_sim_ns"] = now_ns

    def _on_message_lost(self, stream, event) -> None:
        """Count middleware loss reports separately from incomplete groups."""
        self._dds_lost[stream] += event.total_count_change

    # 檢查格式，將 stamp 轉成整數 nanosecond，以 _pending[stamp_ns] 分組。三路到齊才計入 complete_groups
    def _on_image(self, stream: str, msg: Image) -> None:
        """Keep owned ROS messages until all three streams share the same stamp."""
        self._received[stream] += 1
        if self._clock_reset:
            self._blocked_after_reset += 1
            return

        _topic, encoding, frame_id = self.STREAMS[stream]
        bytes_per_pixel = 3 if encoding == "rgb8" else 4
        if (
            msg.encoding != encoding
            or msg.header.frame_id != frame_id
            or msg.width == 0
            or msg.height == 0
            or msg.step < msg.width * bytes_per_pixel
            or len(msg.data) != msg.step * msg.height
            or msg.header.stamp.sec < 0
            or msg.header.stamp.nanosec >= 1_000_000_000
        ):
            self._invalid += 1
            self.get_logger().error(
                f"Invalid {stream} Image: encoding={msg.encoding}, "
                f"frame_id={msg.header.frame_id}, size={msg.width}x{msg.height}, "
                f"step={msg.step}, data_bytes={len(msg.data)}"
            )
            return

        stamp_ns = msg.header.stamp.sec * 1_000_000_000 + msg.header.stamp.nanosec
        self._expire_pending()
        if stamp_ns in self._closed_stamps:
            self._closed_ignored += 1
            return
        if stamp_ns not in self._pending:
            if len(self._pending) >= self._max_pending:
                oldest_stamp = next(iter(self._pending))
                self._discard_group(oldest_stamp, "capacity")
            self._pending[stamp_ns] = {
                "first_seen_sim_ns": self._sim_time_ns,
                "images": {},
            }

        images = self._pending[stamp_ns]["images"]
        if stream in images:
            self._duplicates += 1
            return
        images[stream] = msg
        if len(images) != len(self.STREAMS):
            return

        self._pending.pop(stamp_ns)
        self._closed_stamps.append(stamp_ns)
        self._complete += 1
        # Keep one complete group for inspection; no image copies or disk writes.
        self.latest_group = {
            "stamp_ns": stamp_ns,
            "images": images,
            "received_sim_ns": self._sim_time_ns,
        }
        self._last_age_ns = (
            None if self._sim_time_ns is None else self._sim_time_ns - stamp_ns
        )
        if self._last_age_ns is not None:
            self._max_age_ns = (
                self._last_age_ns
                if self._max_age_ns is None
                else max(self._max_age_ns, self._last_age_ns)
            )

    def _discard_group(self, stamp_ns: int, reason: str) -> None:
        """Close an incomplete observed group and count its absent streams."""
        group = self._pending.pop(stamp_ns)
        self._incomplete += 1
        self._discard_reasons[reason] += 1
        for stream in self.STREAMS:
            if stream not in group["images"]:
                self._missing[stream] += 1
        self._closed_stamps.append(stamp_ns)

    # 未完成組最多保留 10 組，等待上限為 1 秒模擬時間；逾限後統計缺少哪一路。
    def _expire_pending(self) -> None:
        """Bound waiting in simulation time so Pause does not cause expiration."""
        if self._sim_time_ns is None:
            return
        for stamp_ns, group in list(self._pending.items()):
            first_seen = group["first_seen_sim_ns"]
            if first_seen is not None:
                if self._sim_time_ns - first_seen >= self._max_wait_ns:
                    self._discard_group(stamp_ns, "timeout")

    # 輸出配對、DDS 遺失與時間差統計。Pause 期間仍會印統計，等待期限則隨模擬時間暫停。
    def _report(self) -> None:
        """Print one readable block per report, with one value on each line."""
        self._expire_pending()
        latest_stamp = None if self.latest_group is None else self.latest_group["stamp_ns"]
        sim_time_s = (
            "unknown" if self._sim_time_ns is None else f"{self._sim_time_ns / 1e9:.6f}"
        )
        capture_time_s = (
            "unknown" if latest_stamp is None else f"{latest_stamp / 1e9:.6f}"
        )
        age_ms = "unknown" if self._last_age_ns is None else f"{self._last_age_ns / 1e6:.3f}"
        max_age_ms = "unknown" if self._max_age_ns is None else f"{self._max_age_ns / 1e6:.3f}"
        lines = [
            "=" * 48,
            f"sim_time_s              : {sim_time_s}",
            "",
            f"complete_groups         : {self._complete}",
            f"incomplete_groups       : {self._incomplete}",
            f"pending_groups          : {len(self._pending)}",
            f"last_sim_age_ms         : {age_ms}",
            f"max_sim_age_ms          : {max_age_ms}",
            f"dds_lost_total          : {sum(self._dds_lost.values())}",
            f"invalid                 : {self._invalid}",
            f"clock_reset             : {self._clock_reset}",
        ]
        if self.get_parameter("stats_detail").value:
            lines.extend([
                "",
                f"latest_capture_time_s   : {capture_time_s}",
                f"latest_stamp_ns         : {latest_stamp}",
            ])
            for label, counts in (
                ("received", self._received),
                ("missing", self._missing),
                ("dds_lost", self._dds_lost),
            ):
                lines.append("")
                for stream, count in counts.items():
                    lines.append(f"{label + '_' + stream:<24}: {count}")
            lines.extend([
                "",
                f"discard_timeout         : {self._discard_reasons['timeout']}",
                f"discard_capacity        : {self._discard_reasons['capacity']}",
                f"duplicates              : {self._duplicates}",
                f"closed_ignored          : {self._closed_ignored}",
                f"blocked_after_reset     : {self._blocked_after_reset}",
            ])
        lines.append("=" * 48)
        # One log call keeps the ROS prefix outside the field-by-field block.
        self.get_logger().info("\n" + "\n".join(lines))


def main(args=None) -> None:
    """Run the inspection subscriber using the system ROS Python interpreter."""
    rclpy.init(args=args)
    node = None
    try:
        node = DatasetNode()
        rclpy.spin(node)
    except (KeyboardInterrupt, ExternalShutdownException):
        pass
    finally:
        if node is not None:
            node.destroy_node()
        if rclpy.ok():
            rclpy.shutdown()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


【終端機命令】
執行目錄：/home/noel_614420090/reconview

輸出：
import asyncio
import argparse
import sys
import time
import threading
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import rclpy
import omni.timeline
from rclpy.signals import SignalHandlerOptions
from pegasus.simulator.logic.interface.pegasus_interface import PegasusInterface
from pegasus.simulator.params import ROBOTS
from pegasus.simulator.logic.backends.px4_mavlink_backend import (
    PX4MavlinkBackend,
    PX4MavlinkBackendConfig,
)
from pegasus.simulator.logic.graphical_sensors.monocular_camera import MonocularCamera
from pegasus.simulator.logic.vehicles.multirotor import Multirotor, MultirotorConfig
from isaacsim.core.simulation_manager import SimulationManager
from isaacsim.core.utils.stage import open_stage_async
from omni.kit.quicklayout import QuickLayout

project_root = str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1])
if project_root not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, project_root)

from ros2_ws.sim_node.clock import ClockPublisher
from ros2_ws.sim_node.camera import CameraPublisher, CameraReader

# 初始化
async def initialize_pegasus(scene_path):
    pg = PegasusInterface()
    if pg.get_all_vehicles():
        print("Pegasus vehicle already active; initialization skipped")
        return

    print(f"Loading scene: {scene_path}")
    stage_opened, error = await open_stage_async(scene_path)
    if not stage_opened:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to open scene: {error}")
        
    pg.initialize_world()
    await pg.world.initialize_simulation_context_async()
    print("Pegasus World ready")
    stage = pg.world.stage
    if stage.GetPrimAtPath("/World/quadrotor"):
        stage.RemovePrim("/World/quadrotor")

    mavlink_config = PX4MavlinkBackendConfig({
        "vehicle_id": 0,
        "connection_type": "tcpin",
        "connection_ip": "localhost",
        "connection_baseport": 4560,
        "px4_autolaunch": True,
        "px4_dir": pg.px4_path,
        "px4_vehicle_model": pg.px4_default_airframe,
        "enable_lockstep": True,
    })
    backend = PX4MavlinkBackend(mavlink_config)

    vehicle_config = MultirotorConfig()
    vehicle_config.backends = [backend]
    vehicle_config.graphical_sensors = [
        MonocularCamera("fpv_camera"),
        MonocularCamera(
            "top_camera",
            config={
                "position": [0.0, 0.0, -0.03], # 相對於 body 原點的 [x, y, z]，單位為公尺
                "orientation": [0.0, -90.0, 180.0],
                "depth": False,
            },
        ),
    ]

# fpv_camera 預設 /home/noel_614420090/PegasusSimulator/extensions/pegasus.simulator/pegasus/simulator/logic/graphical_sensors/monocular_camera.py
#"position": [0.30, 0.0, 0.0]
# "orientation": [0.0, 0.0, 180.0]
# "depth": True

    Multirotor(
        stage_prefix="/World/quadrotor",
        usd_file=ROBOTS["Iris"],
        vehicle_id=0,
        init_pos=[0.0, 0.0, 0.07],
        init_orientation=[0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0],
        config=vehicle_config,
    )

    """
    Isaac Sim physics step
    → 呼叫 publish_clock()
    → 取得目前模擬時間
    → ClockPublisher 轉換時間格式
    → 發布 /clock
    """
    if not rclpy.ok():
        rclpy.init(args=[], signal_handler_options=SignalHandlerOptions.NO)

    sim_node = rclpy.create_node("sim_node", use_global_arguments=False)
    sim_node.declare_parameter("stats_detail", False)
    clock_publisher = ClockPublisher(sim_node)

    def publish_clock(_dt):
        sim_time = SimulationManager.get_simulation_time()
        clock_publisher.publish_sim_time(sim_time)
        rclpy.spin_once(sim_node, timeout_sec=0.0)

    pg.world.add_physics_callback("publish_clock", publish_clock)
    sim_node.get_logger().info(
        "Publishing Isaac Sim time on /clock at each physics step"
    )
    
    # 前視 Reader 讀取 RGB 與 depth；俯視 Reader 讀取 RGB
    camera_readers = {
        "fpv_camera": CameraReader(
            vehicle_config.graphical_sensors[0], include_depth=True
        ),
        "top_camera": CameraReader(vehicle_config.graphical_sensors[1]),
    }
    camera_output_size = (640, 400)  # 提供給前視與俯視 Publisher
    # 建立前視、俯視兩個 Publisher 輔助物件，共用既有 sim_node
    camera_publishers = {
        "fpv_camera": CameraPublisher(
            sim_node,
            rgb_topic="/fpv_camera/rgb",
            depth_topic="/fpv_camera/depth",
            frame_id="fpv_camera_optical_frame",
            output_size=camera_output_size,
        ),
        "top_camera": CameraPublisher(
            sim_node,
            rgb_topic="/top_camera/rgb",
            frame_id="top_camera_optical_frame",
            output_size=camera_output_size,
        ),
    }

    """
    讀取兩個相機的新 frame
    → 確認兩者 stamp 與 render_reference 相同
    → 距上次發布至少經過 200 ms 模擬時間
    → 發布前視 RGB、前視 depth、俯視 RGB
    """
    camera_publish_period_ns = 200_000_000 # 200 ms 模擬時間
    last_camera_publish_stamp_ns = None # 最近發布那組影像的擷取時間
    camera_published_groups = 0 # 完成三路發布呼叫的組數
    next_camera_log_time = 0.0

    camera_profile_stages = ("read", "resize", "pack", "publish_call")
    camera_cpu_profile = {}

    def reset_camera_profile():
        """Start the next timing window at the first active camera callback."""
        camera_cpu_profile.update(
            window_start_ns=None,
            cpu_ns=0,
            wall_ns=0,
            wall_max_ns=0,
            calls=0,
            published_groups=0,
        )
        for stage_name in camera_profile_stages:
            camera_cpu_profile[f"{stage_name}_cpu_ns"] = 0
            camera_cpu_profile[f"{stage_name}_wall_ns"] = 0

    reset_camera_profile()

    def reset_camera_readers(event):
        nonlocal next_camera_log_time, last_camera_publish_stamp_ns
        nonlocal camera_published_groups
        if event.type in (
            int(omni.timeline.TimelineEventType.PLAY),
            int(omni.timeline.TimelineEventType.PAUSE),
            int(omni.timeline.TimelineEventType.STOP),
        ):
            reset_camera_profile()
            next_camera_log_time = 0.0
        if event.type == int(omni.timeline.TimelineEventType.STOP):
            for reader in camera_readers.values():
                reader.reset()
            last_camera_publish_stamp_ns = None
            camera_published_groups = 0
            next_camera_log_time = 0.0
            sim_node.get_logger().info(
                "Camera acquisition timing, reader state, and publish gate reset after Stop"
            )

    pg.world.add_timeline_callback("reset_camera_readers", reset_camera_readers)

    def read_cameras(_event):
        nonlocal next_camera_log_time, last_camera_publish_stamp_ns
        nonlocal camera_published_groups
        if not pg.world.is_playing():
            return

        camera_work_wall_start_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
        if camera_cpu_profile["window_start_ns"] is None:
            camera_cpu_profile["window_start_ns"] = camera_work_wall_start_ns
            next_camera_log_time = time.monotonic() + 1.0
        camera_work_start_ns = time.thread_time_ns()

        try:
            read_wall_start_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
            read_cpu_start_ns = time.thread_time_ns()
            for reader in camera_readers.values():
                # Reader 等待影像或讀到重複 frame 時，不會重複發布
                reader.read()
            camera_cpu_profile["read_cpu_ns"] += (
                time.thread_time_ns() - read_cpu_start_ns
            )
            camera_cpu_profile["read_wall_ns"] += (
                time.perf_counter_ns() - read_wall_start_ns
            )

            fpv_frame = camera_readers["fpv_camera"].last_frame
            top_frame = camera_readers["top_camera"].last_frame
            readers_ready = all(
                reader.status in ("new_frame", "repeated_frame")
                for reader in camera_readers.values()
            )
            frames_aligned = (
                readers_ready
                and fpv_frame is not None
                and top_frame is not None
                and fpv_frame["stamp_ns"] == top_frame["stamp_ns"]
                and fpv_frame["render_reference"] == top_frame["render_reference"]
            )
            if frames_aligned:
                stamp_ns = fpv_frame["stamp_ns"]
                interval_ready = (
                    last_camera_publish_stamp_ns is None
                    or stamp_ns - last_camera_publish_stamp_ns >= camera_publish_period_ns # 核心判斷
                )
                if interval_ready:
                    for name, frame in (
                        ("fpv_camera", fpv_frame),
                        ("top_camera", top_frame),
                    ):
                        publisher = camera_publishers[name]
                        publisher.publish(frame)
                        for key, value in publisher.last_profile.items():
                            camera_cpu_profile[key] += value
                    last_camera_publish_stamp_ns = stamp_ns
                    # Count completed publish calls, not subscriber deliveries.
                    camera_published_groups += 1
                    camera_cpu_profile["published_groups"] += 1
        except Exception:
            pg.world.remove_render_callback("read_cameras")
            sim_node.get_logger().error(
                "Camera reading and publishing stopped after an error; see traceback"
            )
            raise
        # 每次執行都會累計，包含沒有達到發布間隔的 callback
        camera_cpu_profile["cpu_ns"] += (
            time.thread_time_ns() - camera_work_start_ns
        )
        camera_work_wall_end_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
        camera_work_wall_ns = camera_work_wall_end_ns - camera_work_wall_start_ns
        camera_cpu_profile["wall_ns"] += camera_work_wall_ns
        camera_cpu_profile["wall_max_ns"] = max(
            camera_cpu_profile["wall_max_ns"], camera_work_wall_ns
        )
        camera_cpu_profile["calls"] += 1
        # Wall time only limits console output; capture stamps come from snapshots.
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now < next_camera_log_time:
            return
        next_camera_log_time = now + 1.0
        profile_now_ns = camera_work_wall_end_ns
        window_ns = profile_now_ns - camera_cpu_profile["window_start_ns"]
        cpu_ms = camera_cpu_profile["cpu_ns"] / 1_000_000
        cpu_percent = 100 * camera_cpu_profile["cpu_ns"] / window_ns
        wall_ms = camera_cpu_profile["wall_ns"] / 1_000_000
        wall_max_ms = camera_cpu_profile["wall_max_ns"] / 1_000_000
        show_details = sim_node.get_parameter("stats_detail").value

        rows = [
            ("camera_callback_thread_id", threading.get_native_id()),
            ("profile_window_wall_s", f"{window_ns / 1e9:.3f}"),
            ("camera_calls", camera_cpu_profile["calls"]),
            ("published_groups_window", camera_cpu_profile["published_groups"]),
            ("published_groups_total", camera_published_groups),
            None,
            ("camera_work_cpu_percent", f"{cpu_percent:.3f}"),
            ("camera_work_wall_max_ms", f"{wall_max_ms:.3f}"),
        ]
        if show_details:
            rows.extend([
                ("latest_published_stamp_ns", last_camera_publish_stamp_ns),
                ("camera_work_cpu_ms", f"{cpu_ms:.3f}"),
                ("camera_work_wall_ms", f"{wall_ms:.3f}"),
            ])
        rows.append(None)
        for stage_name in camera_profile_stages:
            stage_cpu_ms = camera_cpu_profile[f"{stage_name}_cpu_ns"] / 1_000_000
            rows.append((f"{stage_name}_cpu_ms", f"{stage_cpu_ms:.3f}"))
            if show_details:
                stage_wall_ms = camera_cpu_profile[f"{stage_name}_wall_ns"] / 1_000_000
                rows.append((f"{stage_name}_wall_ms", f"{stage_wall_ms:.3f}"))
        rows.append(None)

        # Keep the source diagnostics in the same report, outside the work timer.
        if show_details:
            for name, reader in camera_readers.items():
                prefix = "fpv" if name == "fpv_camera" else "top"
                rows.extend([
                    (f"{prefix}_status", reader.status),
                    (f"{prefix}_new_frames", reader.new_frames),
                    (f"{prefix}_repeated_reads", reader.repeated_reads),
                ])
                if reader.status == "waiting_for_render":
                    rows.append((f"{prefix}_wait_detail", reader.wait_detail))
                frame = reader.last_frame
                if frame is not None:
                    rows.extend([
                        (f"{prefix}_capture_time_s", f"{frame['rendering_time']:.6f}"),
                        (f"{prefix}_stamp_ns", frame["stamp_ns"]),
                        (f"{prefix}_dt_ns", frame["dt_ns"]),
                        (f"{prefix}_render_reference", frame["render_reference"]),
                        (f"{prefix}_rgb_shape", frame["rgb"].shape),
                        (f"{prefix}_rgb_dtype", frame["rgb"].dtype),
                    ])
                    depth = frame["depth"]
                    if depth is not None:
                        valid = np.isfinite(depth) & (depth > 0)
                        valid_ratio = np.count_nonzero(valid) / depth.size
                        rows.extend([
                            (f"{prefix}_depth_shape", depth.shape),
                            (f"{prefix}_depth_dtype", depth.dtype),
                            (f"{prefix}_depth_valid_ratio", f"{valid_ratio:.3f}"),
                        ])
                rows.append(None)
            rows.append(("min_publish_interval_ns", camera_publish_period_ns))
        if readers_ready and fpv_frame is not None and top_frame is not None:
            delta_ns = top_frame["stamp_ns"] - fpv_frame["stamp_ns"]
            same_reference = top_frame["render_reference"] == fpv_frame["render_reference"]
            rows.extend([
                ("latest_stamp_delta_ns", delta_ns),
                ("same_render_reference", same_reference),
            ])
        lines = ["=" * 56]
        for row in rows:
            if row is None:
                lines.append("")
            else:
                field_name, value = row
                lines.append(f"{field_name:<30} : {value}")
        lines.append("=" * 56)
        sim_node.get_logger().info("\n" + "\n".join(lines))

        reset_camera_profile()
        camera_cpu_profile["window_start_ns"] = profile_now_ns

    pg.world.add_render_callback("read_cameras", read_cameras)
    sim_node.get_logger().info(
        "Camera snapshot inspection and Image publishing enabled: "
        "/fpv_camera/rgb, /fpv_camera/depth, /top_camera/rgb "
        f"(output {camera_output_size[0]}x{camera_output_size[1]}, "
        "RGB BOX, depth NEAREST, max 5 Hz in simulation time, "
        "sensor data QoS, source capture timestamps). "
        "Timing summaries every second; set stats_detail=true for full diagnostics."
    )

    QuickLayout.quick_load(None, None) # 還原整份已 Quick Save 的視窗配置
    print("Iris and PX4 MAVLink backend created")

parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
parser.add_argument("--scene", required=True)
args = parser.parse_args()

asyncio.ensure_future(initialize_pegasus(args.scene))

#!/usr/bin/env bash

source /opt/ros/jazzy/setup.bash
export ROS_DOMAIN_ID=0
export RMW_IMPLEMENTATION=rmw_fastrtps_cpp
# 緩衝設定
export FASTDDS_BUILTIN_TRANSPORTS="${FASTDDS_BUILTIN_TRANSPORTS:-DEFAULT?sockets_size=33554432}"
echo "ros2 setup complete"
echo "Fast DDS transports: $FASTDDS_BUILTIN_TRANSPORTS"


# source ~/.bashrc
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e

if [[ $# -ne 2 || "$1" != "--scene" ]]; then
    echo "Usage: bash scripts/start_isaac_sim.sh --scene <filename.usd>" >&2
    exit 2
fi

scene_name="$2"

if [[ "$scene_name" == */* ]]; then

from rosgraph_msgs.msg import Clock


class ClockPublisher:
    """Publish supplied simulation timestamps on the ROS 2 clock topic."""

    def __init__(self, node):
        self.publisher = node.create_publisher(Clock, "/clock", 10)

    def publish_sim_time(self, time_seconds: float) -> None:
        """Convert simulation seconds to integer seconds and nanoseconds."""
        total_ns = round(time_seconds * 1_000_000_000)

        msg = Clock()
        msg.clock.sec, msg.clock.nanosec = divmod(
            total_ns, 1_000_000_000
        )

        self.publisher.publish(msg)

輸出：
        images = self._pending[stamp_ns]["images"]
        if stream in images:
            self._duplicates += 1
            return
        images[stream] = msg
        if len(images) != len(self.STREAMS):
            return

        self._pending.pop(stamp_ns)
        self._closed_stamps.append(stamp_ns)
        self._complete += 1
        # Keep one complete group for inspection; no image copies or disk writes.
        self.latest_group = {
            "stamp_ns": stamp_ns,
            "images": images,
            "received_sim_ns": self._sim_time_ns,
        }
        self._last_age_ns = (
            None if self._sim_time_ns is None else self._sim_time_ns - stamp_ns
        )
        if self._last_age_ns is not None:
            self._max_age_ns = (
                self._last_age_ns
                if self._max_age_ns is None
                else max(self._max_age_ns, self._last_age_ns)
            )

    def _discard_group(self, stamp_ns: int, reason: str) -> None:
        """Close an incomplete observed group and count its absent streams."""
        group = self._pending.pop(stamp_ns)
        self._incomplete += 1
        self._discard_reasons[reason] += 1
        for stream in self.STREAMS:
            if stream not in group["images"]:
                self._missing[stream] += 1
        self._closed_stamps.append(stamp_ns)

    # 未完成組最多保留 10 組，等待上限為 1 秒模擬時間；逾限後統計缺少哪一路。
    def _expire_pending(self) -> None:
        """Bound waiting in simulation time so Pause does not cause expiration."""
        if self._sim_time_ns is None:
            return
        for stamp_ns, group in list(self._pending.items()):
            first_seen = group["first_seen_sim_ns"]
            if first_seen is not None:
                if self._sim_time_ns - first_seen >= self._max_wait_ns:
                    self._discard_group(stamp_ns, "timeout")

    # 輸出配對、DDS 遺失與時間差統計。Pause 期間仍會印統計，等待期限則隨模擬時間暫停。
    def _report(self) -> None:
        """Print one readable block per report, with one value on each line."""
        self._expire_pending()
        latest_stamp = None if self.latest_group is None else self.latest_group["stamp_ns"]
        sim_time_s = (
            "unknown" if self._sim_time_ns is None else f"{self._sim_time_ns / 1e9:.6f}"
        )
        capture_time_s = (
            "unknown" if latest_stamp is None else f"{latest_stamp / 1e9:.6f}"
        )
        age_ms = "unknown" if self._last_age_ns is None else f"{self._last_age_ns / 1e6:.3f}"
        max_age_ms = "unknown" if self._max_age_ns is None else f"{self._max_age_ns / 1e6:.3f}"
        lines = [
            "=" * 48,
            f"sim_time_s              : {sim_time_s}",
            "",
            f"complete_groups         : {self._complete}",
            f"incomplete_groups       : {self._incomplete}",
            f"pending_groups          : {len(self._pending)}",
            f"last_sim_age_ms         : {age_ms}",
            f"max_sim_age_ms          : {max_age_ms}",
            f"dds_lost_total          : {sum(self._dds_lost.values())}",
            f"invalid                 : {self._invalid}",
            f"clock_reset             : {self._clock_reset}",
        ]
        if self.get_parameter("stats_detail").value:
            lines.extend([
                "",
                f"latest_capture_time_s   : {capture_time_s}",
                f"latest_stamp_ns         : {latest_stamp}",
            ])
            for label, counts in (
                ("received", self._received),
                ("missing", self._missing),
                ("dds_lost", self._dds_lost),
            ):
                lines.append("")
                for stream, count in counts.items():
                    lines.append(f"{label + '_' + stream:<24}: {count}")
            lines.extend([
                "",
                f"discard_timeout         : {self._discard_reasons['timeout']}",
                f"discard_capacity        : {self._discard_reasons['capacity']}",
                f"duplicates              : {self._duplicates}",
                f"closed_ignored          : {self._closed_ignored}",
                f"blocked_after_reset     : {self._blocked_after_reset}",
            ])
        lines.append("=" * 48)
        # One log call keeps the ROS prefix outside the field-by-field block.
        self.get_logger().info("\n" + "\n".join(lines))


def main(args=None) -> None:
    """Run the inspection subscriber using the system ROS Python interpreter."""
    rclpy.init(args=args)
    node = None
    try:
        node = DatasetNode()
        rclpy.spin(node)
    except (KeyboardInterrupt, ExternalShutdownException):
        pass
    finally:
        if node is not None:
            node.destroy_node()
        if rclpy.ok():
            rclpy.shutdown()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

                policy_name = qos_policy_name_from_kind(event.last_policy_kind)
                logger.warn(
                    "New publisher discovered on topic '{}', offering incompatible QoS. "

                    callback=incompatible_qos_callback,

            event_handlers.append(EventHandler(
                callback_group=callback_group,
                callback=self.liveliness,
                event_type=QoSSubscriptionEventType.RCL_SUBSCRIPTION_LIVELINESS_CHANGED,
                parent_impl=subscription))

            event_handlers.append(EventHandler(
                callback_group=callback_group,
                callback=self.message_lost,
                event_type=QoSSubscriptionEventType.RCL_SUBSCRIPTION_MESSAGE_LOST,
                parent_impl=subscription))

        incompatible_type_callback = None
        if self.incompatible_type:
            incompatible_type_callback = self.incompatible_type
        elif self.use_default_callbacks:
            # Register default callback when not specified
            def _default_incompatible_type_callback(event):
                logger.warn(
                    "Incompatible type on topic '{}', no messages will be sent to it."
                    .format(topic_name))
            incompatible_type_callback = _default_incompatible_type_callback
        try:
            if incompatible_type_callback is not None:
                event_handlers.append(EventHandler(
                    callback_group=callback_group,
                    callback=incompatible_type_callback,
                    event_type=QoSSubscriptionEventType.RCL_SUBSCRIPTION_INCOMPATIBLE_TYPE,
                    parent_impl=subscription))

        except UnsupportedEventTypeError:
            pass

        if self.matched:
            event_handlers.append(EventHandler(
                callback_group=callback_group,
                callback=self.matched,
                event_type=QoSSubscriptionEventType.RCL_SUBSCRIPTION_MATCHED,
                parent_impl=subscription))

        return event_handlers


class PublisherEventCallbacks:
    """Container to provide middleware event callbacks for a Publisher."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        deadline: Optional[Callable[[QoSOfferedDeadlineMissedInfo], None]] = None,
        liveliness: Optional[Callable[[QoSLivelinessLostInfo], None]] = None,
        incompatible_qos: Optional[Callable[[QoSRequestedIncompatibleQoSInfo], None]] = None,
        incompatible_type: Optional[Callable[[IncompatibleTypeInfo], None]] = None,
        matched: Optional[Callable[[QoSPublisherMatchedInfo], None]] = None,
        use_default_callbacks: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Create and return a PublisherEventCallbacks container.

        :param deadline: A user-defined callback that is called when the Publisher misses
            its offered Deadline.
        :param liveliness: A user-defined callback that is called when this Publisher
            fails to signal its Liveliness and is reported as not-alive.
        :param incompatible_qos: A user-defined callback that is called when a Subscription
            with incompatible QoS policies is discovered on subscribed topic.
        :param incompatible_type: A user-defined callback that is called when a topic type
            doesn't match.
        :param matched: A user-defined callback that is called when a Subscription is connected or
            disconnected.
        :param use_default_callbacks: Whether or not to use default callbacks when the user
            doesn't supply one
        """
        self.deadline = deadline
        self.liveliness = liveliness
        self.incompatible_qos = incompatible_qos
        self.incompatible_type = incompatible_type
        self.matched = matched
        self.use_default_callbacks = use_default_callbacks

    def create_event_handlers(
        self, callback_group: CallbackGroup, publisher: _rclpy.Publisher, topic_name: str,
    ) -> List[EventHandler]:
        with publisher:
            logger = get_logger(publisher.get_logger_name())

        event_handlers = []
        if self.deadline:
            event_handlers.append(EventHandler(
                callback_group=callback_group,
                callback=self.deadline,
                event_type=QoSPublisherEventType.RCL_PUBLISHER_OFFERED_DEADLINE_MISSED,
                parent_impl=publisher))

        if self.liveliness:
            event_handlers.append(EventHandler(
                callback_group=callback_group,
                callback=self.liveliness,
                event_type=QoSPublisherEventType.RCL_PUBLISHER_LIVELINESS_LOST,
                parent_impl=publisher))

        incompatible_qos_callback = None
        if self.incompatible_qos:
            incompatible_qos_callback = self.incompatible_qos
        elif self.use_default_callbacks:
            # Register default callback when not specified
            def _default_incompatible_qos_callback(event):
                policy_name = qos_policy_name_from_kind(event.last_policy_kind)
                logger.warn(
                    "New subscription discovered on topic '{}', requesting incompatible QoS. "
                    'No messages will be sent to it. '
                    'Last incompatible policy: {}'.format(topic_name, policy_name))

from enum import Enum
import inspect
from typing import Callable
from typing import TypeVar

from rclpy.callback_groups import CallbackGroup
from rclpy.event_handler import EventHandler
from rclpy.event_handler import SubscriptionEventCallbacks
from rclpy.impl.implementation_singleton import rclpy_implementation as _rclpy
from rclpy.qos import QoSProfile
from rclpy.subscription_content_filter_options import ContentFilterOptions


# Re-export exception defined in _rclpy C extension.
RCLError = _rclpy.RCLError

# For documentation only
MsgType = TypeVar('MsgType')


class Subscription:

    class CallbackType(Enum):
        MessageOnly = 0
        WithMessageInfo = 1

    def __init__(
         self,
         subscription_impl: _rclpy.Subscription,
         msg_type: MsgType,
         topic: str,
         callback: Callable,
         callback_group: CallbackGroup,
         qos_profile: QoSProfile,
         raw: bool,
         event_callbacks: SubscriptionEventCallbacks,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Create a container for a ROS subscription.

        .. warning:: Users should not create a subscription with this constructor, instead they
           should call :meth:`.Node.create_subscription`.

        :param subscription_impl: :class:`Subscription` wrapping the underlying
            ``rcl_subscription_t`` object.
        :param msg_type: The type of ROS messages the subscription will subscribe to.
        :param topic: The name of the topic the subscription will subscribe to.
        :param callback: A user-defined callback function that is called when a message is
            received by the subscription.
        :param callback_group: The callback group for the subscription. If ``None``, then the
            nodes default callback group is used.
        :param qos_profile: The quality of service profile to apply to the subscription.
        :param raw: If ``True``, then received messages will be stored in raw binary
            representation.
        """
        self.__subscription = subscription_impl
        self.msg_type = msg_type
        self.topic = topic
        self.callback = callback
        self.callback_group = callback_group
        # True when the callback is ready to fire but has not been "taken" by an executor
        self._executor_event = False
        self.qos_profile = qos_profile
        self.raw = raw

        self.event_handlers: EventHandler = event_callbacks.create_event_handlers(
            callback_group, subscription_impl, topic)

    def get_publisher_count(self) -> int:
        """Get the number of publishers that this subscription has."""
        with self.handle:
            return self.__subscription.get_publisher_count()

    @property
    def handle(self):
        return self.__subscription

    def destroy(self):
        """
        Destroy a container for a ROS subscription.

        .. warning:: Users should not destroy a subscription with this method, instead they
           should call :meth:`.Node.destroy_subscription`.
        """
        for handler in self.event_handlers:
            handler.destroy()
        self.handle.destroy_when_not_in_use()

    @property
    def topic_name(self):
        with self.handle:
            return self.__subscription.get_topic_name()

    @property
    def callback(self):
        return self._callback

    @callback.setter
    def callback(self, value):
        self._callback = value
        self._callback_type = Subscription.CallbackType.MessageOnly
        try:
            inspect.signature(value).bind(object())
            return
        except TypeError:
            pass
        try:
            inspect.signature(value).bind(object(), object())
            self._callback_type = Subscription.CallbackType.WithMessageInfo
            return
        except TypeError:
            pass
        raise RuntimeError(
            'Subscription.__init__(): callback should be either be callable with one argument'
            '(to get only the message) or two (to get message and message info)')

    @property
    def logger_name(self) -> str:
        """Get the name of the logger associated with the node of the subscription."""
        with self.handle:
            return self.__subscription.get_logger_name()

 * and on remote hosts or processes.

typedef struct RMW_PUBLIC_TYPE rmw_gid_s
{
  /// Name of the rmw implementation
  const char * implementation_identifier;

  /// Byte data GID value
  uint8_t data[RMW_GID_STORAGE_SIZE];
} rmw_gid_t;

#define RMW_MESSAGE_INFO_SEQUENCE_NUMBER_UNSUPPORTED UINT64_MAX

/// Information describing an rmw message
typedef struct RMW_PUBLIC_TYPE rmw_message_info_s
{
  /// Time when the message was published by the publisher.
  /**
   * The exact point at which the timestamp is taken is not specified, but
   * it should be taken consistently at the same point in the
   * publishing process each time.
   */
  rmw_time_point_value_t source_timestamp;
  /// Time when the message was received by the subscription.
  /**
   * The exact point at which the timestamp is taken is not specified, but
   * it should be taken consistently at the same point in the
   * process of receiving a message each time.
   */
  rmw_time_point_value_t received_timestamp;
  /// Sequence number of the received message set by the publisher.
  /**
   * This sequence number is set by the publisher and therefore uniquely identifies
   * a message when combined with the publisher GID.
   * For long running applications, the sequence number might wrap around at some point.
   *
   * If the rmw implementation doesn't support sequence numbers, its value will be
   * RMW_MESSAGE_INFO_SEQUENCE_NUMBER_UNSUPPORTED.
   *
   * Requirements:
   *
   * If `psn1` and `psn2` are the publication sequence numbers obtained by
   * calls to `rmw_take*()`, where `psn1` was obtained in a call that happened before `psn2` and both
   * sequence numbers are from the same publisher (i.e. also same publisher gid), then:
   *
   * - psn2 > psn1 (except in the case of a wrap around)
   * - `psn2 - psn1 - 1` is the number of messages the publisher sent in the middle of both
   *   received messages.
   *   Those might have already been taken by other `rmw_take*()` calls that happened in between or lost.
   *   `psn2 - psn1 - 1 = 0` if and only if the messages were sent by the publisher consecutively.
   */
  uint64_t publication_sequence_number;
  /// Sequence number of the received message set by the subscription.
  /**
   * This sequence number is set by the subscription regardless of which
   * publisher sent the message.
   * For long running applications, the sequence number might wrap around at some point.
   *
   * If the rmw implementation doesn't support sequence numbers, its value will be
   * RMW_MESSAGE_INFO_SEQUENCE_NUMBER_UNSUPPORTED.
   *
   * Requirements:
   *
   * If `rsn1` and `rsn2` are the reception sequence numbers obtained by
   * calls to `rmw_take*()`, where `rsn1` was obtained in a call that happened before `rsn2`, then:
   *
   * - rsn2 > rsn1 (except in the case of a wrap around)
   * - `rsn2 = rsn1 + 1` if and only if both `rmw_take*()` calls happened consecutively.
   */
  uint64_t reception_sequence_number;
  /// Global unique identifier of the publisher that sent the message.
  /**
   * The identifier uniquely identifies the publisher for the local context, but
   * it will not necessarily be the same identifier given in other contexts or processes
   * for the same publisher.
   * Therefore the identifier will uniquely identify the publisher within your application
   * but may disagree about the identifier for that publisher when compared to another
   * application.
   * Even with this limitation, when combined with the publisher sequence number it can
   * uniquely identify a message within your local context.
   * Publisher GIDs generated by the rmw implementation could collide at some point, in which
   * case it is not possible to distinguish which publisher sent the message.
   * The details of how GIDs are generated are rmw implementation dependent.
   *
   * It is possible the the rmw implementation needs to reuse a publisher GID,
   * due to running out of unique identifiers or some other constraint, in which case
   * the rmw implementation may document what happens in that case, but that
   * behavior is not defined here.
   * However, this should be avoided, if at all possible, by the rmw implementation,
   * and should be unlikely to happen in practice.
   *
   * \todo In the future we want this to uniquely identify the publisher globally across
   *   contexts, processes, and machines.
   */
  rmw_gid_t publisher_gid;

  /// Whether this message is from intra_process communication or not
  bool from_intra_process;
} rmw_message_info_t;

"""Inspect exact-time image groups before adding dataset recording."""

from collections import deque
from functools import partial
import math

import rclpy
from rclpy.clock import Clock as RclpyClock
from rclpy.clock_type import ClockType
from rclpy.event_handler import SubscriptionEventCallbacks
from rclpy.executors import ExternalShutdownException
from rclpy.node import Node
from rclpy.qos import QoSProfile, ReliabilityPolicy, qos_profile_sensor_data
from rosgraph_msgs.msg import Clock
from sensor_msgs.msg import Image


class DatasetNode(Node):
    """Match three Image streams by acquisition stamp within one simulation run."""

    STREAMS = {
        "fpv_rgb": ("/fpv_camera/rgb", "rgb8", "fpv_camera_optical_frame"),
        "fpv_depth": ("/fpv_camera/depth", "32FC1", "fpv_camera_optical_frame"),
        "top_rgb": ("/top_camera/rgb", "rgb8", "top_camera_optical_frame"),
    }

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__("dataset_node")
        self.declare_parameter("stats_detail", False)
        self._max_pending = self.declare_parameter("max_pending_groups", 10).value
        wait_seconds = self.declare_parameter("max_wait_sim_sec", 1.0).value
        if self._max_pending <= 0:
            raise ValueError("max_pending_groups must be positive")
        if not math.isfinite(wait_seconds) or wait_seconds <= 0:
            raise ValueError("max_wait_sim_sec must be finite and positive")
        self._max_wait_ns = round(wait_seconds * 1_000_000_000)
        if self._max_wait_ns < 1:
            raise ValueError("max_wait_sim_sec must be at least one nanosecond")

        self._sim_time_ns = None
        self._clock_reset = False
        self._pending = {}
        # Remember recent outcomes so a late message cannot reopen a closed group.
        self._closed_stamps = deque(maxlen=max(100, self._max_pending * 4))
        self.latest_group = None
        self._received = dict.fromkeys(self.STREAMS, 0)
        self._dds_lost = dict.fromkeys(self.STREAMS, 0)
        self._missing = dict.fromkeys(self.STREAMS, 0)
        self._complete = 0
        self._incomplete = 0
        self._discard_reasons = {"timeout": 0, "capacity": 0}
        self._duplicates = 0
        self._closed_ignored = 0
        self._invalid = 0
        self._blocked_after_reset = 0
        self._last_age_ns = None
        self._max_age_ns = None

        clock_qos = QoSProfile(depth=1, reliability=ReliabilityPolicy.BEST_EFFORT)
        self._clock_subscription = self.create_subscription(
            Clock, "/clock", self._on_clock, clock_qos
        )
        self._image_subscriptions = []
        for stream, (topic, _encoding, _frame_id) in self.STREAMS.items():
            subscription = self.create_subscription(
                Image,
                topic,
                partial(self._on_image, stream),
                qos_profile_sensor_data,
                event_callbacks=SubscriptionEventCallbacks(
                    message_lost=partial(self._on_message_lost, stream)
                ),
            )
            self._image_subscriptions.append(subscription)

        # Keep reporting during Pause; expiration uses simulation time only.
        self._report_clock = RclpyClock(clock_type=ClockType.STEADY_TIME)
        self._stats_timer = self.create_timer(
            1.0, self._report, clock=self._report_clock
        )
        self.get_logger().info(
            "Inspecting fpv_rgb/fpv_depth/top_rgb with exact acquisition stamps; "
            f"max_pending_groups={self._max_pending}, "
            f"max_wait_sim_sec={wait_seconds}; depth units=m; "
            "last_sim_age_ms estimates group completion age from the latest /clock"
        )
        # last_sim_age_ms = 最後收到的 /clock − 該完整組的影像擷取時間，這是模擬時間差的估計，會受不同 topic 送達順序影響；先量測數值，再判斷延遲狀況。

    # 更新模擬時間；若時間倒退，清空暫存並停止配對，要求重啟接收端。
    def _on_clock(self, msg: Clock) -> None:
        """Track simulation time and stop pairing if its timeline moves backwards."""
        now_ns = msg.clock.sec * 1_000_000_000 + msg.clock.nanosec
        if (
            not self._clock_reset
            and self._sim_time_ns is not None
            and now_ns < self._sim_time_ns
        ):
            discarded = len(self._pending)
            self._pending.clear()
            self._closed_stamps.clear()
            self.latest_group = None
            self._last_age_ns = None
            self._clock_reset = True
            self.get_logger().error(
                "Simulation time moved backwards; pairing stopped and "
                f"{discarded} pending groups cleared. "
                "Restart dataset_node for a new simulation run."
            )
        self._sim_time_ns = now_ns
        for group in self._pending.values():
            if group["first_seen_sim_ns"] is None:
                group["first_seen_sim_ns"] = now_ns

    def _on_message_lost(self, stream, event) -> None:
        """Count middleware loss reports separately from incomplete groups."""
        self._dds_lost[stream] += event.total_count_change

    # 檢查格式，將 stamp 轉成整數 nanosecond，以 _pending[stamp_ns] 分組。三路到齊才計入 complete_groups
    def _on_image(self, stream: str, msg: Image) -> None:
        """Keep owned ROS messages until all three streams share the same stamp."""
        self._received[stream] += 1
        if self._clock_reset:
            self._blocked_after_reset += 1
            return

        _topic, encoding, frame_id = self.STREAMS[stream]
        bytes_per_pixel = 3 if encoding == "rgb8" else 4
        if (
            msg.encoding != encoding
            or msg.header.frame_id != frame_id
            or msg.width == 0
            or msg.height == 0
            or msg.step < msg.width * bytes_per_pixel
            or len(msg.data) != msg.step * msg.height
            or msg.header.stamp.sec < 0
            or msg.header.stamp.nanosec >= 1_000_000_000
        ):
            self._invalid += 1
            self.get_logger().error(
                f"Invalid {stream} Image: encoding={msg.encoding}, "

from typing import Optional
from typing import TypeVar

import rclpy
from rclpy.event_handler import SubscriptionEventCallbacks
from rclpy.event_handler import UnsupportedEventTypeError
from rclpy.node import Node
from rclpy.qos import QoSDurabilityPolicy
from rclpy.qos import QoSPresetProfiles
from rclpy.qos import QoSProfile
from rclpy.qos import QoSReliabilityPolicy
from rclpy.task import Future
from ros2cli.helpers import unsigned_int
from ros2cli.node.strategy import add_arguments as add_strategy_node_arguments
from ros2cli.node.strategy import NodeStrategy
from ros2topic.api import add_qos_arguments
from ros2topic.api import get_msg_class
from ros2topic.api import positive_float
from ros2topic.api import qos_profile_from_short_keys
from ros2topic.api import TopicNameCompleter
from ros2topic.verb import VerbExtension
from rosidl_runtime_py import message_to_csv
from rosidl_runtime_py import message_to_yaml
from rosidl_runtime_py.utilities import get_message

import yaml

DEFAULT_TRUNCATE_LENGTH = 128
MsgType = TypeVar('MsgType')


class EchoVerb(VerbExtension):
    """Output messages from a topic."""

    def add_arguments(self, parser, cli_name):
        add_strategy_node_arguments(parser)

        arg = parser.add_argument(
            'topic_name',
            help="Name of the ROS topic to listen to (e.g. '/chatter')")
        arg.completer = TopicNameCompleter(
            include_hidden_topics_key='include_hidden_topics')
        parser.add_argument(
            'message_type', nargs='?',
            help="Type of the ROS message (e.g. 'std_msgs/msg/String')")
        add_qos_arguments(parser, 'subscribe', 'sensor_data')
        parser.add_argument(
            '--csv', action='store_true',
            help=(
                'Output all recursive fields separated by commas (e.g. for '
                'plotting). '
                'If --include-message-info is also passed, the following fields are prepended: '
                'source_timestamp, received_timestamp, publication_sequence_number,'
                ' reception_sequence_number.'
            )
        )
        parser.add_argument(
            '--field', type=str, default=None,
            help='Echo a selected field of a message. '
                 "Use '.' to select sub-fields. "
                 'For example, to echo the position field of a nav_msgs/msg/Odometry message: '
                 "'ros2 topic echo /odom --field pose.pose.position'",
        )
        parser.add_argument(
            '--full-length', '-f', action='store_true',
            help='Output all elements for arrays, bytes, and string with a '
                 "length > '--truncate-length', by default they are truncated "
                 "after '--truncate-length' elements with '...''")
        parser.add_argument(
            '--truncate-length', '-l', type=unsigned_int, default=DEFAULT_TRUNCATE_LENGTH,
            help='The length to truncate arrays, bytes, and string to '
                 '(default: %d)' % DEFAULT_TRUNCATE_LENGTH)
        parser.add_argument(
            '--no-arr', action='store_true', help="Don't print array fields of messages")
        parser.add_argument(
            '--no-str', action='store_true', help="Don't print string fields of messages")
        parser.add_argument(
            '--flow-style', action='store_true',
            help='Print collections in the block style (not available with csv format)')
        parser.add_argument(
            '--no-lost-messages', action='store_true', help="Don't report when a message is lost")
        parser.add_argument(
            '--raw', action='store_true', help='Echo the raw binary representation')
        parser.add_argument(
            '--filter', dest='filter_expr', help='Python expression to filter messages that '
                                                 'are printed. Expression can use Python builtins '
                                                 'as well as m (the message).')
        parser.add_argument(
            '--once', action='store_true', help='Print the first message received and then exit.')
        parser.add_argument(
            '--timeout', metavar='N', type=positive_float,
            help='Set a timeout in seconds for waiting', default=None)
        parser.add_argument(
            '--include-message-info', '-i', action='store_true',
            help='Shows the associated message info.')

    def choose_qos(self, node, args):

        if (args.qos_reliability is not None or
                args.qos_durability is not None or
                args.qos_depth is not None or
                args.qos_history is not None or
                args.qos_liveliness is not None or
                args.qos_liveliness_lease_duration_seconds is not None):

            return qos_profile_from_short_keys(
                args.qos_profile,
                reliability=args.qos_reliability,
                durability=args.qos_durability,
                depth=args.qos_depth,
                history=args.qos_history,
                liveliness=args.qos_liveliness,
                liveliness_lease_duration_s=args.qos_liveliness_lease_duration_seconds)

        qos_profile = QoSPresetProfiles.get_from_short_key(args.qos_profile)
        reliability_reliable_endpoints_count = 0
