## 一、專案目標

由使用者逐步建立無人機導航研究系統，涵蓋 Isaac Sim、PX4、ROS 2，以及後續的資料蒐集、模型訓練與 Closed-loop Evaluation。使用者應能解釋、修改與驗證核心流程；可使用官方套件與範例。

## 二、架構與命名決定權

- **專案架構、資料夾結構、檔案名稱與位置，以及模組和套件的劃分，全部由使用者決定。** AI 僅提供選項、理由與影響分析，除非使用者明確委託該次決策。
- 新增、拆分、合併、搬移或重新命名前，先提出具體方案，由使用者決定後再執行；此規則在工程模式下也適用。
- 不在範例或指令中暗自加入未決定的結構與檔名。已決定的項目持續沿用，不重複詢問或擅自調整。
- 工具若有限制或會自動產生檔案，先說明限制，由使用者決定可設定的輸出位置與命名規則，之後依規則執行。
- 可以建議檔案或資料夾名命名以及架構設計。


## 三、工作模式與教學
- 工作模式: 帶我驗證，告知修改內容，代碼解釋。
- **預設採教學模式：使用者編寫與執行，AI 負責說明、提示、審查與協助除錯。** 不自行代寫完整程式、建立專案骨架、覆寫成果或執行開發與測試；已有明確授權的範圍依授權進行。
- 每次聚焦一個小目標：說明必要觀念 → 使用者實作 → 審查與驗證。遇到困難先提示，再給最小範例，不一次提供大量程式碼或待辦。
- 不預設使用者熟悉程式語法。說明設計原因、輸入輸出、主要流程與驗證方式，以實際操作確認理解；概念問題可直接回答，不強制安排練習。
- 「解釋、分析、比較、規劃、檢查」預設唯讀；使用者明確要求直接實作或指定工程模式時，完成最小必要修改與適當驗證，並說明結果。
- 工作模式依每次任務判斷；已授權的必要步驟不重複確認。會影響目標或介面的關鍵歧義，先提出精簡問題。
- 說明使用繁體中文與台灣用語，專有名詞保留英文，例如 ENU、NED、Behavior Cloning；程式碼、識別名稱、註解與 Docstring 一律使用英文。完整提示詞放在獨立、可複製的文字區塊。

## 四、漸進開發

- 一次主要增加一個可驗證的功能，先完成最小流程，再依具體需求增加架構、依賴與自動化。
- 不預先建立完整框架或加入未採用的演算法，不為簡單功能新增不必要的 Node、Wrapper 或抽象層；提議新增檔案或依賴時，說明必要性。
- 舊專案可供對照，不直接複製整套架構；沿用片段前說明用途、相依條件與驗證方式。引用第三方程式須保留適用的授權與來源。
- 文件保留必要的設定、決策與證據，不為每個小改動新增文件，也不要求使用者一次規劃整份專案。

## 五、技術介面與驗證

- 串接元件時，明確定義資料格式、單位、Coordinate Frame、Timestamp 與更新頻率。涉及 ENU、NED、Body Frame 或 World Frame 轉換時，以簡單案例驗證方向與正負號。
- 修改控制命令、模型輸入輸出或時間對齊方式時，檢查上下游相容性。依改動選擇直接相關的驗證；飛控與系統整合不得只憑語法檢查宣稱正確。
- 區分 Windows 本機、Ubuntu Server 與模擬環境的路徑及執行位置，不假設已連到使用者的 Server。版本與 API 以所選版本的官方文件為準，不套用舊專案設定。
- 模擬驗證不等於實機可用；實機操作須有明確任務範圍與相應驗證。

## 六、研究紀錄與操作範圍

- 區分已確認事實、待驗證假說與未知事項，優先用最小測試區分原因。回報時區分已完成、未執行、建議操作與尚待驗證。
- 區分 Training、Validation、Test、Offline Evaluation 與 Closed-loop Evaluation；不以 Training Loss 或 Offline Error 下降宣稱泛化能力或飛行成功。
- 實驗保存必要的 Dataset、Episode、Split、Seed、Hyperparameters、Checkpoint 與程式版本。評估前定義成功、碰撞與超時條件，不事後放寬條件、隱藏失敗或忽略例外錯誤。
- 不修改無關檔案或使用者的未提交變更，不擅自刪除或覆寫舊專案、資料集、模型與實驗紀錄；舊結果不得當作新專案已驗證的成果。
- 大量資料蒐集、昂貴訓練、批次模擬，以及 Git Commit、Push、建立分支或破壞性操作，須在使用者授權範圍內進行。
- 不將密碼、Token 或 SSH Private Key 寫入程式、文件或輸出。

## 七、終端機狀態與統計輸出

- 供人閱讀的週期性狀態與統計，預設使用多行區塊，前後以 `=` 分隔線包住。
- 每行顯示一個欄位，使用 `field_name : value`，固定欄位順序並對齊冒號；相關欄位之間可用空白行分組。
- 欄位名稱使用英文，時間、距離等數值須標示單位，例如 `_s`、`_ms`、`_ns`、`_m`。
- Dictionary 或多路統計應拆成個別欄位，避免在同一行展開大量內容。
- 同一次統計以一次 logger 呼叫輸出完整區塊；週期性摘要預設每秒一次，避免每個 frame 或 physics step 都輸出完整區塊。
- 錯誤保留原有嚴重程度與 traceback；終端機排版不得改變資料內容、時間戳記或統計意義。
- 此規則適用於人閱讀的終端機輸出；機器讀取與研究紀錄依其選定格式保存。

"""Inspect image delivery and Linux hardware load without recording images.

Run with the system ROS 2 Jazzy Python environment on the Isaac Sim host.
Start before Play, wait for one matched publisher per topic, then measure.
Pause and let counters settle before comparing them with the publisher.

Sequence gaps cover only the interval between received messages from the same
publisher. They cannot locate a loss in the network, DDS history, or executor,
or detect losses before the first or after the last received message.
Capture timestamp gaps alone are not evidence of transport loss.
"""

from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from dataclasses import dataclass
from functools import partial
from pathlib import Path
import os
import socket
import subprocess
import time

import rclpy
from rclpy.clock import Clock as RclpyClock
from rclpy.clock_type import ClockType
from rclpy.event_handler import SubscriptionEventCallbacks, UnsupportedEventTypeError
from rclpy.executors import ExternalShutdownException
from rclpy.node import Node
from rclpy.qos import QoSProfile, ReliabilityPolicy
from rosgraph_msgs.msg import Clock
from sensor_msgs.msg import Image


STREAMS = {
    "fpv_rgb": ("/fpv_camera/rgb", "rgb8", "fpv_camera_optical_frame", 3),
    "fpv_depth": ("/fpv_camera/depth", "32FC1", "fpv_camera_optical_frame", 4),
    "top_rgb": ("/top_camera/rgb", "rgb8", "top_camera_optical_frame", 3),
}
UNSUPPORTED_SEQUENCE = (1 << 64) - 1


@dataclass
class StreamStats:
    """Track callbacks and publication sequences without retaining image data."""

    received: int = 0
    invalid: int = 0
    dds_lost: int | None = 0
    last_stamp_ns: int | None = None
    max_capture_gap_ns: int = 0
    stamp_regressions: int = 0
    repeated_stamps: int = 0
    publisher_gid: bytes | None = None
    last_sequence: int | None = None
    sequence_samples: int = 0
    unsupported_samples: int = 0
    sequence_anomalies: int = 0
    publisher_changes: int = 0
    missing_between_received: int = 0

    @property
    def sequence_status(self) -> str:
        if self.publisher_changes or self.sequence_anomalies:
            return "invalid_comparison"
        if self.unsupported_samples:
            return "partial_metadata" if self.sequence_samples else "unavailable"
        return "available" if self.sequence_samples >= 2 else "waiting_for_samples"

    def observe_sequence(self, info: dict) -> None:
        """Compare sequences only within one publisher identity.

        The sequence contract and unsupported sentinel are defined in ROS 2 rmw:
        https://github.com/ros2/rmw/blob/jazzy/rmw/include/rmw/types.h
        """
        sequence = info.get("publication_sequence_number")
        gid = bytes(info.get("publisher_gid") or b"")
        if (
            not isinstance(sequence, int)
            or not 0 <= sequence < UNSUPPORTED_SEQUENCE
            or not any(gid)
        ):
            self.unsupported_samples += 1
            # Do not bridge an interval whose metadata was unavailable.
            self.last_sequence = None
            return
        if self.publisher_gid is not None and gid != self.publisher_gid:
            self.publisher_changes += 1
            self.last_sequence = None
        self.publisher_gid = gid
        if self.last_sequence is not None:
            if sequence > self.last_sequence:
                self.missing_between_received += sequence - self.last_sequence - 1
            else:
                self.sequence_anomalies += 1
        self.last_sequence = sequence
        self.sequence_samples += 1

    def observe_stamp(self, stamp_ns: int) -> None:
        if self.last_stamp_ns is not None:
            difference = stamp_ns - self.last_stamp_ns
            if difference < 0:
                self.stamp_regressions += 1
            elif difference == 0:
                self.repeated_stamps += 1
            else:
                self.max_capture_gap_ns = max(self.max_capture_gap_ns, difference)
        self.last_stamp_ns = stamp_ns


def read_cpu_ticks() -> dict:
    """Read aggregate and logical CPU ticks, excluding duplicate guest fields."""
    ticks = {}
    for line in Path("/proc/stat").read_text().splitlines():
        fields = line.split()
        if fields and fields[0].startswith("cpu"):
            values = [int(value) for value in fields[1:9]]
            ticks[fields[0]] = (sum(values), values[3] + values[4])
    return ticks


def read_key_values(path: str) -> dict:
    return {
        fields[0].rstrip(":"): int(fields[1])
        for line in Path(path).read_text().splitlines()
        if len(fields := line.split()) >= 2
    }


def sample_hardware(previous) -> tuple:
    """Sample host resources in a worker so nvidia-smi cannot block ROS callbacks."""
    now_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
    cpu = read_cpu_ticks()
    memory = read_key_values("/proc/meminfo")
    vm = read_key_values("/proc/vmstat")
    rows = []
    if previous is not None:
        previous_ns, previous_cpu, previous_vm = previous
        elapsed_s = (now_ns - previous_ns) / 1e9
        busy = {}
        for name, (total, idle) in cpu.items():
            if name in previous_cpu:
                old_total, old_idle = previous_cpu[name]
                delta = total - old_total
                if delta > 0:
                    busy[name] = 100 * (delta - (idle - old_idle)) / delta
        rows.append(("cpu_total_percent", f"{busy.get('cpu', 0.0):.1f}"))
        logical = [value for name, value in busy.items() if name != "cpu"]
        rows.append(("cpu_busiest_logical_percent", f"{max(logical, default=0.0):.1f}"))
        page_mib = os.sysconf("SC_PAGE_SIZE") / (1024 * 1024)
        for name, key in (("swap_in_mib_s", "pswpin"), ("swap_out_mib_s", "pswpout")):
            rate = (vm[key] - previous_vm[key]) * page_mib / elapsed_s
            rows.append((name, f"{rate:.3f}"))
    else:
        rows.extend((name, "warming_up") for name in (
            "cpu_total_percent", "cpu_busiest_logical_percent",
            "swap_in_mib_s", "swap_out_mib_s",
        ))
    rows.extend([
        ("ram_total_mib", f"{memory['MemTotal'] / 1024:.1f}"),
        ("ram_available_mib", f"{memory['MemAvailable'] / 1024:.1f}"),
        ("swap_used_mib", f"{(memory['SwapTotal'] - memory['SwapFree']) / 1024:.1f}"),
    ])
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            ["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=index,utilization.gpu,memory.used,memory.total",
             "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"],
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=2.0, check=False,
        )
    except FileNotFoundError:
        rows.append(("gpu_status", "nvidia_smi_not_found"))
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        rows.append(("gpu_status", "nvidia_smi_timeout"))
    else:
        rows.append(("gpu_status", "available" if result.returncode == 0 else "query_failed"))
        if result.returncode:
            rows.append(("gpu_query_error", " ".join(result.stderr.split())))
        else:
            for line in result.stdout.splitlines():
                index, utilization, used, total = [field.strip() for field in line.split(",")]
                rows.extend([
                    (f"gpu{index}_util_percent", utilization),
                    (f"gpu{index}_memory_used_mib", used),
                    (f"gpu{index}_memory_total_mib", total),
                ])
    return (now_ns, cpu, vm), rows


class TransportCheck(Node):
    """Observe the existing three topics with the same best-effort queue depth."""

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__("transport_check")
        depth = self.declare_parameter("queue_depth", 5).value
        if type(depth) is not int or depth <= 0:
            raise ValueError("queue_depth must be a positive integer")
        self.declare_parameter("stats_detail", False)
        monitor_hardware = self.declare_parameter("monitor_hardware", True).value
        self.streams = {name: StreamStats() for name in STREAMS}
        self.clock_ns = None
        self.clock_reset = False
        self.last_clock_advance_ns = None
        self.start_ns = self.previous_report_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
        self.previous_clock_ns = None
        self.previous_received = dict.fromkeys(STREAMS, 0)
        self.pending = {}
        self.complete_groups = 0
        self.evicted_unmatched_groups = 0
        self.blocked_after_reset = 0
        self._hardware_previous = None
        self._hardware_rows = [("hardware_status", "warming_up" if monitor_hardware else "disabled")]
        self._hardware_pool = None
        self._hardware_future = None
        self.subscriptions_by_stream = {}
        qos = QoSProfile(depth=depth, reliability=ReliabilityPolicy.BEST_EFFORT)
        for stream, (topic, _encoding, _frame_id, _bytes) in STREAMS.items():
            try:
                subscription = self.create_subscription(
                    Image, topic, partial(self._on_image, stream), qos,
                    event_callbacks=SubscriptionEventCallbacks(
                        message_lost=partial(self._on_lost, stream)
                    ),
                )
            except UnsupportedEventTypeError:
                self.streams[stream].dds_lost = None
                self.get_logger().warning(
                    f"Message-lost events unsupported for {topic}; DDS loss count is unknown"
                )
                subscription = self.create_subscription(
                    Image, topic, partial(self._on_image, stream), qos
                )
            self.subscriptions_by_stream[stream] = subscription
        self.clock_subscription = self.create_subscription(
            Clock, "/clock", self._on_clock,
            QoSProfile(depth=1, reliability=ReliabilityPolicy.BEST_EFFORT),
        )
        self._steady_clock = RclpyClock(clock_type=ClockType.STEADY_TIME)
        self._timer = self.create_timer(1.0, self.report, clock=self._steady_clock)
        if monitor_hardware:
            self._hardware_pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1)
            self._hardware_future = self._hardware_pool.submit(sample_hardware, None)
        self.get_logger().info(
            f"Host={socket.gethostname()}, PID={os.getpid()}, "
            f"ROS_DOMAIN_ID={os.environ.get('ROS_DOMAIN_ID', '0')}, "
            f"RMW={rclpy.get_rmw_implementation_identifier()}, queue_depth={depth}. "
            "Read-only diagnostic; no image copies or disk recording. "
            "Sequence gaps exclude startup/tail loss and do not identify the loss layer. "
            "Hardware load is for this host; run here only if Isaac Sim runs here."
        )

    def _on_lost(self, stream: str, event) -> None:
        self.streams[stream].dds_lost += event.total_count_change

    def _on_clock(self, msg: Clock) -> None:
        now_ns = msg.clock.sec * 1_000_000_000 + msg.clock.nanosec
        if self.clock_ns is not None and now_ns < self.clock_ns and not self.clock_reset:
            self.clock_reset = True
            self.get_logger().error(
                "Simulation clock moved backwards; image measurement stopped. "
                "Restart this checker for the next run."
            )
        if self.clock_ns is None or now_ns > self.clock_ns:
            self.last_clock_advance_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
        self.clock_ns = now_ns

    def _on_image(self, stream: str, msg: Image, info: dict) -> None:
        if self.clock_reset:
            self.blocked_after_reset += 1
            return
        stats = self.streams[stream]
        stats.received += 1
        stats.observe_sequence(info)
        _topic, encoding, frame_id, bytes_per_pixel = STREAMS[stream]
        if (
            msg.encoding != encoding or msg.header.frame_id != frame_id
            or msg.width == 0 or msg.height == 0
            or msg.step < msg.width * bytes_per_pixel
            or len(msg.data) != msg.height * msg.step
            or msg.header.stamp.sec < 0
            or not 0 <= msg.header.stamp.nanosec < 1_000_000_000
        ):
            stats.invalid += 1
            self.get_logger().error(f"Invalid Image metadata or payload length on {stream}")
            return
        stamp_ns = msg.header.stamp.sec * 1_000_000_000 + msg.header.stamp.nanosec
        stats.observe_stamp(stamp_ns)
        # Retain only stamps and stream names. No timeout is inferred during Pause.
        streams = self.pending.setdefault(stamp_ns, set())
        streams.add(stream)
        if len(streams) == len(STREAMS):
            self.complete_groups += 1
            del self.pending[stamp_ns]
        if len(self.pending) > 256:
            del self.pending[next(iter(self.pending))]
            self.evicted_unmatched_groups += 1

    def report(self, *, final: bool = False) -> None:
        """Emit one block; wall rates and observed simulation progress stay separate."""
        now_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
        wall_s = (now_ns - self.previous_report_ns) / 1e9
        ratio = "unknown"
        if self.previous_clock_ns is not None and not self.clock_reset:
            ratio = f"{(self.clock_ns - self.previous_clock_ns) / 1e9 / wall_s:.3f}"
        counts = {
            name: subscription.get_publisher_count()
            for name, subscription in self.subscriptions_by_stream.items()
        }
        clock_publishers = self.clock_subscription.get_publisher_count()
        ready = all(count == 1 for count in counts.values()) and clock_publishers == 1
        if self._hardware_future is not None and self._hardware_future.done():
            # Unexpected sampling errors propagate with their original traceback.
            self._hardware_previous, self._hardware_rows = self._hardware_future.result()
            self._hardware_future = None
        hardware_age = (
            "unknown" if self._hardware_previous is None
            else f"{(now_ns - self._hardware_previous[0]) / 1e9:.3f}"
        )
        rows = [
            ("report_type", "final" if final else "periodic"),
            ("elapsed_wall_s", f"{(now_ns - self.start_ns) / 1e9:.3f}"),
            ("window_wall_s", f"{wall_s:.3f}"),
            ("publishers_ready", ready),
            ("clock_publishers", clock_publishers),
            ("sim_wall_ratio_est", ratio),
            ("clock_idle_wall_s", "unknown" if self.last_clock_advance_ns is None
             else f"{(now_ns - self.last_clock_advance_ns) / 1e9:.3f}"),
            ("clock_reset", self.clock_reset),
            None,
            ("complete_groups_total", self.complete_groups),
            ("unmatched_observed_groups", len(self.pending)),
            ("evicted_unmatched_groups", self.evicted_unmatched_groups),
        ]
        for name, stats in self.streams.items():
            rate = (stats.received - self.previous_received[name]) / wall_s
            missing = stats.missing_between_received if stats.sequence_status == "available" else "unknown"
            rows.extend([
                None,
                (f"{name}_publishers", counts[name]),
                (f"{name}_received_total", stats.received),
                (f"{name}_receive_hz_wall", f"{rate:.3f}"),
                (f"{name}_seq_status", stats.sequence_status),
                (f"{name}_seq_missing_total", missing),
                (f"{name}_dds_lost_total", "unknown" if stats.dds_lost is None else stats.dds_lost),
                (f"{name}_invalid_total", stats.invalid),
            ])
            if self.get_parameter("stats_detail").value:
                rows.extend([
                    (f"{name}_last_stamp_ns", stats.last_stamp_ns),
                    (f"{name}_capture_gap_max_ms", f"{stats.max_capture_gap_ns / 1e6:.3f}"),
                    (f"{name}_stamp_regressions", stats.stamp_regressions),
                    (f"{name}_repeated_stamps", stats.repeated_stamps),
                    (f"{name}_publisher_changes", stats.publisher_changes),
                    (f"{name}_sequence_anomalies", stats.sequence_anomalies),
                ])
            self.previous_received[name] = stats.received
        rows.extend([None, ("hardware_sample_age_s", hardware_age), *self._hardware_rows])
        lines = ["=" * 64]
        lines.extend("" if row is None else f"{row[0]:<36} : {row[1]}" for row in rows)
        lines.append("=" * 64)
        self.get_logger().info("\n" + "\n".join(lines))
        self.previous_report_ns = now_ns
        self.previous_clock_ns = self.clock_ns
        if self._hardware_pool is not None and self._hardware_future is None and not final:
            self._hardware_future = self._hardware_pool.submit(sample_hardware, self._hardware_previous)

    def close_hardware(self) -> None:
        if self._hardware_pool is not None:
            self._hardware_pool.shutdown(wait=True, cancel_futures=True)


def main(args=None) -> None:
    rclpy.init(args=args)
    node = None
    try:
        node = TransportCheck()
        rclpy.spin(node)
    except (KeyboardInterrupt, ExternalShutdownException):
        pass
    finally:
        if node is not None:
            try:
                if rclpy.ok():
                    node.report(final=True)
            finally:
                node.close_hardware()
                node.destroy_node()
        if rclpy.ok():
            rclpy.shutdown()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""Inspect image delivery and Linux hardware load without recording images.

Run with the system ROS 2 Jazzy Python environment on the Isaac Sim host.
Start before Play, wait for one matched publisher per topic, then measure.
Pause and let counters settle before comparing them with the publisher.

Sequence gaps cover only the interval between received messages from the same
publisher. They cannot locate a loss in the network, DDS history, or executor,
or detect losses before the first or after the last received message.
Jazzy does not expose a per-message publisher GID in Python; graph identity and
matched events guard the required single-publisher measurement interval.
Capture timestamp gaps alone are not evidence of transport loss.
"""

from collections import deque
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from dataclasses import dataclass
from functools import partial
from pathlib import Path
import os
import socket
import subprocess
import time

import rclpy
from rclpy.clock import Clock as RclpyClock
from rclpy.clock_type import ClockType
from rclpy.event_handler import SubscriptionEventCallbacks, UnsupportedEventTypeError
from rclpy.executors import ExternalShutdownException
from rclpy.node import Node
from rclpy.qos import QoSProfile, ReliabilityPolicy
from rclpy.signals import SignalHandlerOptions
from rosgraph_msgs.msg import Clock
from sensor_msgs.msg import Image


STREAMS = {
    "fpv_rgb": ("/fpv_camera/rgb", "rgb8", "fpv_camera_optical_frame", 3),
    "fpv_depth": ("/fpv_camera/depth", "32FC1", "fpv_camera_optical_frame", 4),
    "top_rgb": ("/top_camera/rgb", "rgb8", "top_camera_optical_frame", 3),
}
UNSUPPORTED_SEQUENCE = (1 << 64) - 1


@dataclass
class StreamStats:
    """Track callbacks and publication sequences without retaining image data."""

    received: int = 0
    invalid: int = 0
    dds_lost: int | None = 0
    last_stamp_ns: int | None = None
    max_capture_gap_ns: int = 0
    stamp_regressions: int = 0
    repeated_stamps: int = 0
    publisher_gid: bytes | None = None
    last_sequence: int | None = None
    sequence_samples: int = 0
    unsupported_samples: int = 0
    sequence_anomalies: int = 0
    publisher_changes: int = 0
    missing_between_received: int = 0
    matched_events_supported: bool = True

    @property
    def sequence_status(self) -> str:
        if not self.matched_events_supported:
            return "publisher_guard_unavailable"
        if self.publisher_changes or self.sequence_anomalies:
            return "invalid_comparison"
        if self.unsupported_samples:
            return "partial_metadata" if self.sequence_samples else "unavailable"
        return "available" if self.sequence_samples >= 2 else "waiting_for_samples"

    def observe_sequence(self, info: dict) -> None:
        """Compare sequences only within one publisher identity.

        The sequence contract and unsupported sentinel are defined in ROS 2 rmw:
        https://github.com/ros2/rmw/blob/jazzy/rmw/include/rmw/types.h
        """
        sequence = info.get("publication_sequence_number")
        gid = bytes(info.get("publisher_gid") or b"")
        if (
            not isinstance(sequence, int)
            or not 0 <= sequence < UNSUPPORTED_SEQUENCE
            or not any(gid)
        ):
            self.unsupported_samples += 1
            # Do not bridge an interval whose metadata was unavailable.
            self.last_sequence = None
            return
        if self.publisher_gid is not None and gid != self.publisher_gid:
            self.publisher_changes += 1
            self.last_sequence = None
        self.publisher_gid = gid
        if self.last_sequence is not None:
            if sequence > self.last_sequence:
                self.missing_between_received += sequence - self.last_sequence - 1
            else:
                self.sequence_anomalies += 1
        self.last_sequence = max(sequence, self.last_sequence) if self.last_sequence is not None else sequence
        self.sequence_samples += 1

    def observe_stamp(self, stamp_ns: int) -> None:
        if self.last_stamp_ns is not None:
            difference = stamp_ns - self.last_stamp_ns
            if difference < 0:
                self.stamp_regressions += 1
            elif difference == 0:
                self.repeated_stamps += 1
            else:
                self.max_capture_gap_ns = max(self.max_capture_gap_ns, difference)
        self.last_stamp_ns = stamp_ns


def read_cpu_ticks() -> dict:
    """Read aggregate and logical CPU ticks, excluding duplicate guest fields."""
    ticks = {}
    for line in Path("/proc/stat").read_text().splitlines():
        fields = line.split()
        if fields and fields[0].startswith("cpu"):
            values = [int(value) for value in fields[1:9]]
            ticks[fields[0]] = (sum(values), values[3] + values[4])
    return ticks


def read_key_values(path: str) -> dict:
    return {
        fields[0].rstrip(":"): int(fields[1])
        for line in Path(path).read_text().splitlines()
        if len(fields := line.split()) >= 2
    }


def sample_hardware(previous) -> tuple:
    """Sample host resources in a worker so nvidia-smi cannot block ROS callbacks."""
    now_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
    cpu = read_cpu_ticks()
    memory = read_key_values("/proc/meminfo")
    vm = read_key_values("/proc/vmstat")
    rows = []
    if previous is not None:
        previous_ns, previous_cpu, previous_vm = previous
        elapsed_s = (now_ns - previous_ns) / 1e9
        busy = {}
        for name, (total, idle) in cpu.items():
            if name in previous_cpu:
                old_total, old_idle = previous_cpu[name]
                delta = total - old_total
                if delta > 0:
                    busy[name] = 100 * (delta - (idle - old_idle)) / delta
        rows.append(("cpu_total_percent", f"{busy.get('cpu', 0.0):.1f}"))
        logical = [value for name, value in busy.items() if name != "cpu"]
        rows.append(("cpu_busiest_logical_percent", f"{max(logical, default=0.0):.1f}"))
        page_mib = os.sysconf("SC_PAGE_SIZE") / (1024 * 1024)
        for name, key in (("swap_in_mib_s", "pswpin"), ("swap_out_mib_s", "pswpout")):
            rate = (vm[key] - previous_vm[key]) * page_mib / elapsed_s
            rows.append((name, f"{rate:.3f}"))
    else:
        rows.extend((name, "warming_up") for name in (
            "cpu_total_percent", "cpu_busiest_logical_percent",
            "swap_in_mib_s", "swap_out_mib_s",
        ))
    rows.extend([
        ("ram_total_mib", f"{memory['MemTotal'] / 1024:.1f}"),
        ("ram_available_mib", f"{memory['MemAvailable'] / 1024:.1f}"),
        ("swap_used_mib", f"{(memory['SwapTotal'] - memory['SwapFree']) / 1024:.1f}"),
    ])
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            ["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=index,utilization.gpu,memory.used,memory.total",
             "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"],
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=2.0, check=False,
        )
    except FileNotFoundError:
        rows.append(("gpu_status", "nvidia_smi_not_found"))
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        rows.append(("gpu_status", "nvidia_smi_timeout"))
    else:
        rows.append(("gpu_status", "available" if result.returncode == 0 else "query_failed"))
        if result.returncode:
            rows.append(("gpu_query_error", " ".join(result.stderr.split())))
        else:
            for line in result.stdout.splitlines():
                index, utilization, used, total = [field.strip() for field in line.split(",")]
                rows.extend([
                    (f"gpu{index}_util_percent", utilization),
                    (f"gpu{index}_memory_used_mib", used),
                    (f"gpu{index}_memory_total_mib", total),
                ])
    return (now_ns, cpu, vm), rows


class TransportCheck(Node):
    """Observe the existing three topics with the same best-effort queue depth."""

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__("transport_check")
        depth = self.declare_parameter("queue_depth", 5).value
        if type(depth) is not int or depth <= 0:
            raise ValueError("queue_depth must be a positive integer")
        self.declare_parameter("stats_detail", False)
        monitor_hardware = self.declare_parameter("monitor_hardware", True).value
        self.streams = {name: StreamStats() for name in STREAMS}
        self.clock_ns = None
        self.clock_reset = False
        self.last_clock_advance_ns = None
        self.start_ns = self.previous_report_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
        self.previous_clock_ns = None
        self.previous_received = dict.fromkeys(STREAMS, 0)
        self.pending = {}
        self.closed_stamps = deque(maxlen=256)
        self.complete_groups = 0
        self.evicted_unmatched_groups = 0
        self.blocked_after_reset = 0
        self._hardware_previous = None
        self._hardware_rows = [("hardware_status", "warming_up" if monitor_hardware else "disabled")]
        self._hardware_pool = None
        self._hardware_future = None
        self.subscriptions_by_stream = {}
        qos = QoSProfile(depth=depth, reliability=ReliabilityPolicy.BEST_EFFORT)
        for stream, (topic, _encoding, _frame_id, _bytes) in STREAMS.items():
            try:
                subscription = self.create_subscription(
                    Image, topic, partial(self._on_image, stream), qos,
                    event_callbacks=SubscriptionEventCallbacks(
                        message_lost=partial(self._on_lost, stream),
                        matched=partial(self._on_matched, stream),
                    ),
                )
            except UnsupportedEventTypeError:
                self.streams[stream].dds_lost = None
                self.streams[stream].matched_events_supported = False
                self.get_logger().warning(
                    f"Subscription events unsupported for {topic}; "
                    "DDS loss and guarded sequence comparison are unknown"
                )
                subscription = self.create_subscription(
                    Image, topic, partial(self._on_image, stream), qos
                )


【終端機命令】
執行目錄：/home/noel_614420090/reconview
狀態：completed
結束代碼：0
命令：
/bin/bash -lc "sed -n '241,480p' ros2_ws/dataset_node/check/tarnsport.py"
輸出：
            self.subscriptions_by_stream[stream] = subscription
        self.clock_subscription = self.create_subscription(
            Clock, "/clock", self._on_clock,
            QoSProfile(depth=1, reliability=ReliabilityPolicy.BEST_EFFORT),
        )
        self._steady_clock = RclpyClock(clock_type=ClockType.STEADY_TIME)
        self._timer = self.create_timer(1.0, self.report, clock=self._steady_clock)
        if monitor_hardware:
            self._hardware_pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1)
            self._hardware_future = self._hardware_pool.submit(sample_hardware, None)
        self.get_logger().info(
            f"Host={socket.gethostname()}, PID={os.getpid()}, "
            f"ROS_DOMAIN_ID={self.context.get_domain_id()}, "
            f"RMW={rclpy.get_rmw_implementation_identifier()}, queue_depth={depth}. "
            "Read-only diagnostic; no image copies or disk recording. "
            "Sequence gaps exclude startup/tail loss and do not identify the loss layer. "
            "Python publisher identity is guarded by graph data and matched events. "
            "Hardware load is for this host; run here only if Isaac Sim runs here."
        )

    def _on_lost(self, stream: str, event) -> None:
        self.streams[stream].dds_lost += event.total_count_change

    def _on_matched(self, stream: str, event) -> None:
        # A second match invalidates the entire interval, even after a disconnect.
        stats = self.streams[stream]
        stats.publisher_changes = max(stats.publisher_changes, event.total_count - 1)

    def _on_clock(self, msg: Clock) -> None:
        now_ns = msg.clock.sec * 1_000_000_000 + msg.clock.nanosec
        if self.clock_ns is not None and now_ns < self.clock_ns and not self.clock_reset:
            self.clock_reset = True
            self.get_logger().error(
                "Simulation clock moved backwards; image measurement stopped. "
                "Restart this checker for the next run."
            )
        if self.clock_ns is None or now_ns > self.clock_ns:
            self.last_clock_advance_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
        self.clock_ns = now_ns

    def _on_image(self, stream: str, msg: Image, info: dict) -> None:
        if self.clock_reset:
            self.blocked_after_reset += 1
            return
        stats = self.streams[stream]
        stats.received += 1
        _topic, encoding, frame_id, bytes_per_pixel = STREAMS[stream]
        publishers = self.get_publishers_info_by_topic(_topic)
        metadata = dict(info)
        if len(publishers) == 1:
            metadata.setdefault("publisher_gid", bytes(publishers[0].endpoint_gid))
        else:
            # Never compare sequences while identity is ambiguous or undiscovered.
            metadata.pop("publisher_gid", None)
            if len(publishers) > 1:
                stats.publisher_changes = max(stats.publisher_changes, 1)
        stats.observe_sequence(metadata)
        if (
            msg.encoding != encoding or msg.header.frame_id != frame_id
            or msg.width == 0 or msg.height == 0
            or msg.step < msg.width * bytes_per_pixel
            or len(msg.data) != msg.height * msg.step
            or msg.header.stamp.sec < 0
            or not 0 <= msg.header.stamp.nanosec < 1_000_000_000
        ):
            stats.invalid += 1
            self.get_logger().error(f"Invalid Image metadata or payload length on {stream}")
            return
        stamp_ns = msg.header.stamp.sec * 1_000_000_000 + msg.header.stamp.nanosec
        stats.observe_stamp(stamp_ns)
        # Retain only stamps and stream names. No timeout is inferred during Pause.
        if stamp_ns in self.closed_stamps:
            return
        streams = self.pending.setdefault(stamp_ns, set())
        streams.add(stream)
        if len(streams) == len(STREAMS):
            self.complete_groups += 1
            del self.pending[stamp_ns]
            self.closed_stamps.append(stamp_ns)
        if len(self.pending) > 256:
            del self.pending[next(iter(self.pending))]
            self.evicted_unmatched_groups += 1

    def report(self, *, final: bool = False) -> None:
        """Emit one block; wall rates and observed simulation progress stay separate."""
        now_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
        wall_s = (now_ns - self.previous_report_ns) / 1e9
        ratio = "unknown"
        if self.previous_clock_ns is not None and not self.clock_reset:
            ratio = f"{(self.clock_ns - self.previous_clock_ns) / 1e9 / wall_s:.3f}"
        counts = {
            name: subscription.get_publisher_count()
            for name, subscription in self.subscriptions_by_stream.items()
        }
        clock_publishers = self.clock_subscription.get_publisher_count()
        ready = all(count == 1 for count in counts.values()) and clock_publishers == 1
        if self._hardware_future is not None and self._hardware_future.done():
            # Unexpected sampling errors propagate with their original traceback.
            self._hardware_previous, self._hardware_rows = self._hardware_future.result()
            self._hardware_future = None
        hardware_age = (
            "unknown" if self._hardware_previous is None
            else f"{(now_ns - self._hardware_previous[0]) / 1e9:.3f}"
        )
        rows = [
            ("report_type", "final" if final else "periodic"),
            ("elapsed_wall_s", f"{(now_ns - self.start_ns) / 1e9:.3f}"),
            ("window_wall_s", f"{wall_s:.3f}"),
            ("publishers_ready", ready),
            ("clock_publishers", clock_publishers),
            ("sim_wall_ratio_est", ratio),
            ("clock_idle_wall_s", "unknown" if self.last_clock_advance_ns is None
             else f"{(now_ns - self.last_clock_advance_ns) / 1e9:.3f}"),
            ("clock_reset", self.clock_reset),
            ("blocked_after_reset", self.blocked_after_reset),
            None,
            ("complete_groups_total", self.complete_groups),
            ("unmatched_observed_groups", len(self.pending)),
            ("evicted_unmatched_groups", self.evicted_unmatched_groups),
        ]
        for name, stats in self.streams.items():
            rate = (stats.received - self.previous_received[name]) / wall_s
            missing = stats.missing_between_received if stats.sequence_status == "available" else "unknown"
            rows.extend([
                None,
                (f"{name}_publishers", counts[name]),
                (f"{name}_received_total", stats.received),
                (f"{name}_receive_hz_wall", f"{rate:.3f}"),
                (f"{name}_seq_status", stats.sequence_status),
                (f"{name}_seq_missing_total", missing),
                (f"{name}_dds_lost_total", "unknown" if stats.dds_lost is None else stats.dds_lost),
                (f"{name}_invalid_total", stats.invalid),
            ])
            if self.get_parameter("stats_detail").value:
                rows.extend([
                    (f"{name}_last_stamp_ns", stats.last_stamp_ns),
                    (f"{name}_capture_gap_max_ms", f"{stats.max_capture_gap_ns / 1e6:.3f}"),
                    (f"{name}_stamp_regressions", stats.stamp_regressions),
                    (f"{name}_repeated_stamps", stats.repeated_stamps),
                    (f"{name}_publisher_changes", stats.publisher_changes),
                    (f"{name}_sequence_anomalies", stats.sequence_anomalies),
                ])
            self.previous_received[name] = stats.received
        rows.extend([None, ("hardware_sample_age_s", hardware_age), *self._hardware_rows])
        lines = ["=" * 64]
        lines.extend("" if row is None else f"{row[0]:<36} : {row[1]}" for row in rows)
        lines.append("=" * 64)
        self.get_logger().info("\n" + "\n".join(lines))
        self.previous_report_ns = now_ns
        self.previous_clock_ns = self.clock_ns
        if self._hardware_pool is not None and self._hardware_future is None and not final:
            self._hardware_future = self._hardware_pool.submit(sample_hardware, self._hardware_previous)

    def close_hardware(self) -> None:
        if self._hardware_pool is not None:
            self._hardware_pool.shutdown(wait=True, cancel_futures=True)


def main(args=None) -> None:
    # Preserve Python's KeyboardInterrupt so a final report can precede shutdown.
    rclpy.init(args=args, signal_handler_options=SignalHandlerOptions.NO)
    node = None
    try:
        node = TransportCheck()
        rclpy.spin(node)
    except (KeyboardInterrupt, ExternalShutdownException):
        pass
    finally:
        if node is not None:
            try:
                if rclpy.ok():
                    node.report(final=True)
            finally:
                node.close_hardware()
                node.destroy_node()
        if rclpy.ok():
            rclpy.shutdown()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""Inspect image delivery and Linux hardware load without recording images.

Run with the system ROS 2 Jazzy Python environment on the Isaac Sim host.
Start before Play, wait for one matched publisher per topic, then measure.
Pause and let counters settle before comparing them with the publisher.

Sequence gaps cover only the interval between received messages from the same
publisher. They cannot locate a loss in the network, DDS history, or executor,
or detect losses before the first or after the last received message.
Jazzy does not expose a per-message publisher GID in Python; graph identity and
matched events guard the required single-publisher measurement interval.
Capture timestamp gaps alone are not evidence of transport loss.
"""

from collections import deque
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from dataclasses import dataclass
from functools import partial
from pathlib import Path
import os
import socket
import subprocess
import time

import rclpy
from rclpy.clock import Clock as RclpyClock
from rclpy.clock_type import ClockType
from rclpy.event_handler import SubscriptionEventCallbacks, UnsupportedEventTypeError
from rclpy.executors import ExternalShutdownException
from rclpy.node import Node
from rclpy.qos import QoSProfile, ReliabilityPolicy
from rclpy.signals import SignalHandlerOptions
from rosgraph_msgs.msg import Clock
from sensor_msgs.msg import Image


STREAMS = {
    "fpv_rgb": ("/fpv_camera/rgb", "rgb8", "fpv_camera_optical_frame", 3),
    "fpv_depth": ("/fpv_camera/depth", "32FC1", "fpv_camera_optical_frame", 4),
    "top_rgb": ("/top_camera/rgb", "rgb8", "top_camera_optical_frame", 3),
}
UNSUPPORTED_SEQUENCE = (1 << 64) - 1


@dataclass
class StreamStats:
    """Track callbacks and publication sequences without retaining image data."""

    received: int = 0
    invalid: int = 0
    dds_lost: int | None = 0
    last_stamp_ns: int | None = None
    max_capture_gap_ns: int = 0
    stamp_regressions: int = 0
    repeated_stamps: int = 0
    publisher_gid: bytes | None = None
    last_sequence: int | None = None
    sequence_samples: int = 0
    unsupported_samples: int = 0
    sequence_anomalies: int = 0
    publisher_changes: int = 0
    missing_between_received: int = 0
    matched_events_supported: bool = True

    @property
    def sequence_status(self) -> str:
        if not self.matched_events_supported:
            return "publisher_guard_unavailable"
        if self.publisher_changes or self.sequence_anomalies:
            return "invalid_comparison"
        if self.unsupported_samples:
            return "partial_metadata" if self.sequence_samples else "unavailable"
        return "available" if self.sequence_samples >= 2 else "waiting_for_samples"

    def observe_sequence(self, info: dict) -> None:
        """Compare sequences only within one publisher identity.

        The sequence contract and unsupported sentinel are defined in ROS 2 rmw:
        https://github.com/ros2/rmw/blob/jazzy/rmw/include/rmw/types.h
        """
        sequence = info.get("publication_sequence_number")
        gid = bytes(info.get("publisher_gid") or b"")
        if (
            not isinstance(sequence, int)
            or not 0 <= sequence < UNSUPPORTED_SEQUENCE
            or not any(gid)
        ):
            self.unsupported_samples += 1
            # Do not bridge an interval whose metadata was unavailable.
            self.last_sequence = None
            return
        if self.publisher_gid is not None and gid != self.publisher_gid:
            self.publisher_changes += 1
            self.last_sequence = None
        self.publisher_gid = gid
        if self.last_sequence is not None:
            if sequence > self.last_sequence:
                self.missing_between_received += sequence - self.last_sequence - 1
            else:
                self.sequence_anomalies += 1
        self.last_sequence = max(sequence, self.last_sequence) if self.last_sequence is not None else sequence
        self.sequence_samples += 1

    def observe_stamp(self, stamp_ns: int) -> None:
        if self.last_stamp_ns is not None:
            difference = stamp_ns - self.last_stamp_ns
            if difference < 0:
                self.stamp_regressions += 1
            elif difference == 0:
                self.repeated_stamps += 1
            else:
                self.max_capture_gap_ns = max(self.max_capture_gap_ns, difference)
        self.last_stamp_ns = stamp_ns


def read_cpu_ticks() -> dict:
    """Read aggregate and logical CPU ticks, excluding duplicate guest fields."""
    ticks = {}
    for line in Path("/proc/stat").read_text().splitlines():
        fields = line.split()
        if fields and fields[0].startswith("cpu"):
            values = [int(value) for value in fields[1:9]]
            ticks[fields[0]] = (sum(values), values[3] + values[4])
    return ticks


def read_key_values(path: str) -> dict:
    return {
        fields[0].rstrip(":"): int(fields[1])
        for line in Path(path).read_text().splitlines()

                rows.extend([
                    (f"gpu{index}_util_percent", utilization),
                    (f"gpu{index}_memory_used_mib", used),
                    (f"gpu{index}_memory_total_mib", total),
                ])
    return (now_ns, cpu, vm), rows


class TransportCheck(Node):
    """Observe the existing three topics with the same best-effort queue depth."""

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__("transport_check")
        depth = self.declare_parameter("queue_depth", 5).value
        if type(depth) is not int or depth <= 0:
            raise ValueError("queue_depth must be a positive integer")
        self.declare_parameter("stats_detail", False)
        monitor_hardware = self.declare_parameter("monitor_hardware", True).value
        self.streams = {name: StreamStats() for name in STREAMS}
        self.clock_ns = None
        self.clock_reset = False
        self.last_clock_advance_ns = None
        self.start_ns = self.previous_report_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
        self.previous_clock_ns = None
        self.previous_received = dict.fromkeys(STREAMS, 0)
        self.pending = {}
        self.closed_stamps = deque(maxlen=256)
        self.complete_groups = 0
        self.evicted_unmatched_groups = 0
        self.blocked_after_reset = 0
        self._hardware_previous = None
        self._hardware_rows = [("hardware_status", "warming_up" if monitor_hardware else "disabled")]
        self._hardware_pool = None
        self._hardware_future = None
        self.subscriptions_by_stream = {}
        qos = QoSProfile(depth=depth, reliability=ReliabilityPolicy.BEST_EFFORT)
        for stream, (topic, _encoding, _frame_id, _bytes) in STREAMS.items():
            try:
                subscription = self.create_subscription(
                    Image, topic, partial(self._on_image, stream), qos,
                    event_callbacks=SubscriptionEventCallbacks(
                        message_lost=partial(self._on_lost, stream),
                        matched=partial(self._on_matched, stream),
                    ),
                )
            except UnsupportedEventTypeError:
                self.streams[stream].dds_lost = None
                self.streams[stream].matched_events_supported = False
                self.get_logger().warning(
                    f"Subscription events unsupported for {topic}; "
                    "DDS loss and guarded sequence comparison are unknown"
                )
                subscription = self.create_subscription(
                    Image, topic, partial(self._on_image, stream), qos
                )
            self.subscriptions_by_stream[stream] = subscription
        self.clock_subscription = self.create_subscription(
            Clock, "/clock", self._on_clock,
            QoSProfile(depth=1, reliability=ReliabilityPolicy.BEST_EFFORT),
        )
        self._steady_clock = RclpyClock(clock_type=ClockType.STEADY_TIME)
        self._timer = self.create_timer(1.0, self.report, clock=self._steady_clock)
        if monitor_hardware:
            self._hardware_pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1)
            self._hardware_future = self._hardware_pool.submit(sample_hardware, None)
        self.get_logger().info(
            f"Host={socket.gethostname()}, PID={os.getpid()}, "
            f"ROS_DOMAIN_ID={self.context.get_domain_id()}, "
            f"RMW={rclpy.get_rmw_implementation_identifier()}, queue_depth={depth}. "
            "Read-only diagnostic; no image copies or disk recording. "
            "Sequence gaps exclude startup/tail loss and do not identify the loss layer. "
            "Python publisher identity is guarded by graph data and matched events. "
            "Hardware load is for this host; run here only if Isaac Sim runs here."
        )

    def _on_lost(self, stream: str, event) -> None:
        self.streams[stream].dds_lost += event.total_count_change

    def _on_matched(self, stream: str, event) -> None:
        # A second match invalidates the entire interval, even after a disconnect.
        stats = self.streams[stream]
        stats.publisher_changes = max(stats.publisher_changes, event.total_count - 1)

    def _on_clock(self, msg: Clock) -> None:
        now_ns = msg.clock.sec * 1_000_000_000 + msg.clock.nanosec
        if self.clock_ns is not None and now_ns < self.clock_ns and not self.clock_reset:
            self.clock_reset = True
            self.get_logger().error(
                "Simulation clock moved backwards; image measurement stopped. "
                "Restart this checker for the next run."
            )
        if self.clock_ns is None or now_ns > self.clock_ns:
            self.last_clock_advance_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
        self.clock_ns = now_ns

    def _on_image(self, stream: str, msg: Image, info: dict) -> None:
        if self.clock_reset:
            self.blocked_after_reset += 1
            return
        stats = self.streams[stream]
        stats.received += 1
        _topic, encoding, frame_id, bytes_per_pixel = STREAMS[stream]
        publishers = self.get_publishers_info_by_topic(_topic)
        metadata = dict(info)
        if len(publishers) == 1:
            metadata.setdefault("publisher_gid", bytes(publishers[0].endpoint_gid))
        else:
            # Never compare sequences while identity is ambiguous or undiscovered.
            metadata.pop("publisher_gid", None)
            if len(publishers) > 1:
                stats.publisher_changes = max(stats.publisher_changes, 1)
        stats.observe_sequence(metadata)
        if (
            msg.encoding != encoding or msg.header.frame_id != frame_id
            or msg.width == 0 or msg.height == 0
            or msg.step < msg.width * bytes_per_pixel
            or len(msg.data) != msg.height * msg.step
            or msg.header.stamp.sec < 0
            or not 0 <= msg.header.stamp.nanosec < 1_000_000_000
        ):
            stats.invalid += 1
            self.get_logger().error(f"Invalid Image metadata or payload length on {stream}")
            return
        stamp_ns = msg.header.stamp.sec * 1_000_000_000 + msg.header.stamp.nanosec
        stats.observe_stamp(stamp_ns)
        # Retain only stamps and stream names. No timeout is inferred during Pause.
        if stamp_ns in self.closed_stamps:
            return
        streams = self.pending.setdefault(stamp_ns, set())
        streams.add(stream)
        if len(streams) == len(STREAMS):
            self.complete_groups += 1
            del self.pending[stamp_ns]
            self.closed_stamps.append(stamp_ns)
        if len(self.pending) > 256:
            del self.pending[next(iter(self.pending))]
            self.evicted_unmatched_groups += 1

    def report(self, *, final: bool = False) -> None:
        """Emit one block; wall rates and observed simulation progress stay separate."""
        now_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
        wall_s = (now_ns - self.previous_report_ns) / 1e9
        ratio = "unknown"
        if self.previous_clock_ns is not None and not self.clock_reset:
            ratio = f"{(self.clock_ns - self.previous_clock_ns) / 1e9 / wall_s:.3f}"
        counts = {
            name: subscription.get_publisher_count()
            for name, subscription in self.subscriptions_by_stream.items()
        }
        clock_publishers = self.clock_subscription.get_publisher_count()
        ready = all(count == 1 for count in counts.values()) and clock_publishers == 1
        if self._hardware_future is not None and self._hardware_future.done():
            # Unexpected sampling errors propagate with their original traceback.
            self._hardware_previous, self._hardware_rows = self._hardware_future.result()
            self._hardware_future = None
        hardware_age = (
            "unknown" if self._hardware_previous is None
            else f"{(now_ns - self._hardware_previous[0]) / 1e9:.3f}"
        )
        rows = [
            ("report_type", "final" if final else "periodic"),
            ("elapsed_wall_s", f"{(now_ns - self.start_ns) / 1e9:.3f}"),
            ("window_wall_s", f"{wall_s:.3f}"),
            ("publishers_ready", ready),
            ("clock_publishers", clock_publishers),
            ("sim_wall_ratio_est", ratio),
            ("clock_idle_wall_s", "unknown" if self.last_clock_advance_ns is None
             else f"{(now_ns - self.last_clock_advance_ns) / 1e9:.3f}"),
            ("clock_reset", self.clock_reset),
            ("blocked_after_reset", self.blocked_after_reset),
            None,
            ("complete_groups_total", self.complete_groups),
            ("unmatched_observed_groups", len(self.pending)),
            ("evicted_unmatched_groups", self.evicted_unmatched_groups),
        ]
        for name, stats in self.streams.items():
            rate = (stats.received - self.previous_received[name]) / wall_s
            missing = stats.missing_between_received if stats.sequence_status == "available" else "unknown"
            rows.extend([
                None,
                (f"{name}_publishers", counts[name]),
                (f"{name}_received_total", stats.received),
                (f"{name}_receive_hz_wall", f"{rate:.3f}"),
                (f"{name}_seq_status", stats.sequence_status),
                (f"{name}_seq_missing_total", missing),
                (f"{name}_dds_lost_total", "unknown" if stats.dds_lost is None else stats.dds_lost),
                (f"{name}_invalid_total", stats.invalid),
            ])
            if self.get_parameter("stats_detail").value:
                rows.extend([
                    (f"{name}_last_stamp_ns", stats.last_stamp_ns),
                    (f"{name}_capture_gap_max_ms", f"{stats.max_capture_gap_ns / 1e6:.3f}"),
                    (f"{name}_stamp_regressions", stats.stamp_regressions),
                    (f"{name}_repeated_stamps", stats.repeated_stamps),
                    (f"{name}_publisher_changes", stats.publisher_changes),
                    (f"{name}_sequence_anomalies", stats.sequence_anomalies),
                ])
            self.previous_received[name] = stats.received
        rows.extend([None, ("hardware_sample_age_s", hardware_age), *self._hardware_rows])
        lines = ["=" * 64]
        lines.extend("" if row is None else f"{row[0]:<36} : {row[1]}" for row in rows)
        lines.append("=" * 64)
        self.get_logger().info("\n" + "\n".join(lines))
        self.previous_report_ns = now_ns
        self.previous_clock_ns = self.clock_ns
        if self._hardware_pool is not None and self._hardware_future is None and not final:
            self._hardware_future = self._hardware_pool.submit(sample_hardware, self._hardware_previous)

    def close_hardware(self) -> None:
        if self._hardware_pool is not None:
            self._hardware_pool.shutdown(wait=True, cancel_futures=True)


def main(args=None) -> None:
    # Preserve Python's KeyboardInterrupt so a final report can precede shutdown.
    rclpy.init(args=args, signal_handler_options=SignalHandlerOptions.NO)
    node = None
    try:
        node = TransportCheck()
        rclpy.spin(node)
    except (KeyboardInterrupt, ExternalShutdownException):
        pass
    finally:
        if node is not None:
            try:
                if rclpy.ok():
                    node.report(final=True)
            finally:
                node.close_hardware()
                node.destroy_node()
        if rclpy.ok():
            rclpy.shutdown()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

   269	    def _on_clock(self, msg: Clock) -> None:
   270	        now_ns = msg.clock.sec * 1_000_000_000 + msg.clock.nanosec
   271	        if self.clock_ns is not None and now_ns < self.clock_ns and not self.clock_reset:
   272	            self.clock_reset = True
   273	            self.get_logger().error(
   274	                "Simulation clock moved backwards; image measurement stopped. "
   275	                "Restart this checker for the next run."
   276	            )
   277	        if self.clock_ns is None or now_ns > self.clock_ns:
   278	            self.last_clock_advance_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
   279	        self.clock_ns = now_ns
   280	
   281	    def _on_image(self, stream: str, msg: Image, info: dict) -> None:
   282	        if self.clock_reset:
   283	            self.blocked_after_reset += 1
   284	            return
   285	        stats = self.streams[stream]
   286	        stats.received += 1
   287	        _topic, encoding, frame_id, bytes_per_pixel = STREAMS[stream]
   288	        publishers = self.get_publishers_info_by_topic(_topic)
   289	        metadata = dict(info)
   290	        if len(publishers) == 1:
   291	            metadata.setdefault("publisher_gid", bytes(publishers[0].endpoint_gid))
   292	        else:
   293	            # Never compare sequences while identity is ambiguous or undiscovered.
   294	            metadata.pop("publisher_gid", None)
   295	            if len(publishers) > 1:
   296	                stats.publisher_changes = max(stats.publisher_changes, 1)
   297	        stats.observe_sequence(metadata)
   298	        if (
   299	            msg.encoding != encoding or msg.header.frame_id != frame_id
   300	            or msg.width == 0 or msg.height == 0
   301	            or msg.step < msg.width * bytes_per_pixel
   302	            or len(msg.data) != msg.height * msg.step
   303	            or msg.header.stamp.sec < 0
   304	            or not 0 <= msg.header.stamp.nanosec < 1_000_000_000
   305	        ):
   306	            stats.invalid += 1
   307	            self.get_logger().error(f"Invalid Image metadata or payload length on {stream}")
   308	            return
   309	        stamp_ns = msg.header.stamp.sec * 1_000_000_000 + msg.header.stamp.nanosec
   310	        stats.observe_stamp(stamp_ns)
   311	        # Retain only stamps and stream names. No timeout is inferred during Pause.
   312	        if stamp_ns in self.closed_stamps:
   313	            return
   314	        streams = self.pending.setdefault(stamp_ns, set())
   315	        streams.add(stream)
   316	        if len(streams) == len(STREAMS):
   317	            self.complete_groups += 1
   318	            del self.pending[stamp_ns]
   319	            self.closed_stamps.append(stamp_ns)
   320	        if len(self.pending) > 256:
   321	            del self.pending[next(iter(self.pending))]
   322	            self.evicted_unmatched_groups += 1

   324	    def report(self, *, final: bool = False) -> None:
   325	        """Emit one block; wall rates and observed simulation progress stay separate."""
   326	        show_details = self.get_parameter("stats_detail").value
   327	        now_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
   328	        wall_s = (now_ns - self.previous_report_ns) / 1e9
   329	        ratio = "unknown"
   330	        if self.previous_clock_ns is not None and not self.clock_reset:
   331	            ratio = f"{(self.clock_ns - self.previous_clock_ns) / 1e9 / wall_s:.3f}"
   332	        counts = {
   333	            name: subscription.get_publisher_count()
   334	            for name, subscription in self.subscriptions_by_stream.items()
   335	        }
   336	        clock_publishers = self.clock_subscription.get_publisher_count()
   337	        ready = all(count == 1 for count in counts.values()) and clock_publishers == 1
   338	        if self._hardware_future is not None and self._hardware_future.done():
   339	            # Unexpected sampling errors propagate with their original traceback.
   340	            self._hardware_previous, self._hardware_rows = self._hardware_future.result()
   341	            self._hardware_future = None
   342	        hardware_age = (
   343	            "unknown" if self._hardware_previous is None
   344	            else f"{(now_ns - self._hardware_previous[0]) / 1e9:.3f}"
   345	        )

【終端機命令】
執行目錄：/home/noel_614420090/reconview

命令：
/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba ros2_ws/dataset_node/check/tarnsport.py | sed -n '1,200p'"
輸出：
     1	"""Inspect image delivery and Linux hardware load without recording images.
     2	
     3	Run with the system ROS 2 Jazzy Python environment on the Isaac Sim host.
     4	Start before Play, wait for one matched publisher per topic, then measure.
     5	Pause and let counters settle before comparing them with the publisher.
     6	
     7	Sequence gaps cover only the interval between received messages from the same
     8	publisher. They cannot locate a loss in the network, DDS history, or executor,
     9	or detect losses before the first or after the last received message.
    10	Jazzy does not expose a per-message publisher GID in Python; graph identity and
    11	matched events guard the required single-publisher measurement interval.
    12	Capture timestamp gaps alone are not evidence of transport loss.
    13	"""
    14	
    15	from collections import deque
    16	from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
    17	from dataclasses import dataclass
    18	from functools import partial
    19	from pathlib import Path
    20	import os
    21	import socket
    22	import subprocess
    23	import time
    24	
    25	import rclpy
    26	from rclpy.clock import Clock as RclpyClock
    27	from rclpy.clock_type import ClockType
    28	from rclpy.event_handler import SubscriptionEventCallbacks, UnsupportedEventTypeError
    29	from rclpy.executors import ExternalShutdownException
    30	from rclpy.node import Node
    31	from rclpy.qos import QoSProfile, ReliabilityPolicy
    32	from rclpy.signals import SignalHandlerOptions
    33	from rosgraph_msgs.msg import Clock
    34	from sensor_msgs.msg import Image
    35	
    36	
    37	STREAMS = {
    38	    "fpv_rgb": ("/fpv_camera/rgb", "rgb8", "fpv_camera_optical_frame", 3),
    39	    "fpv_depth": ("/fpv_camera/depth", "32FC1", "fpv_camera_optical_frame", 4),
    40	    "top_rgb": ("/top_camera/rgb", "rgb8", "top_camera_optical_frame", 3),
    41	}
    42	UNSUPPORTED_SEQUENCE = (1 << 64) - 1
    43	
    44	
    45	@dataclass
    46	class StreamStats:
    47	    """Track callbacks and publication sequences without retaining image data."""
    48	
    49	    received: int = 0
    50	    invalid: int = 0
    51	    dds_lost: int | None = 0
    52	    last_stamp_ns: int | None = None
    53	    max_capture_gap_ns: int = 0
    54	    stamp_regressions: int = 0
    55	    repeated_stamps: int = 0
    56	    publisher_gid: bytes | None = None
    57	    last_sequence: int | None = None
    58	    sequence_samples: int = 0
    59	    unsupported_samples: int = 0
    60	    sequence_anomalies: int = 0
    61	    publisher_changes: int = 0
    62	    missing_between_received: int = 0
    63	    matched_events_supported: bool = True
    64	
    65	    @property
    66	    def sequence_status(self) -> str:
    67	        if not self.matched_events_supported:
    68	            return "publisher_guard_unavailable"
    69	        if self.publisher_changes or self.sequence_anomalies:
    70	            return "invalid_comparison"
    71	        if self.unsupported_samples:
    72	            return "partial_metadata" if self.sequence_samples else "unavailable"
    73	        return "available" if self.sequence_samples >= 2 else "waiting_for_samples"
    74	
    75	    def observe_sequence(self, info: dict) -> None:
    76	        """Compare sequences only within one publisher identity.
    77	
    78	        The sequence contract and unsupported sentinel are defined in ROS 2 rmw:
    79	        https://github.com/ros2/rmw/blob/jazzy/rmw/include/rmw/types.h
    80	        """
    81	        sequence = info.get("publication_sequence_number")
    82	        gid = bytes(info.get("publisher_gid") or b"")
    83	        if (
    84	            not isinstance(sequence, int)
    85	            or not 0 <= sequence < UNSUPPORTED_SEQUENCE
    86	            or not any(gid)
    87	        ):
    88	            self.unsupported_samples += 1
    89	            # Do not bridge an interval whose metadata was unavailable.
    90	            self.last_sequence = None
    91	            return
    92	        if self.publisher_gid is not None and gid != self.publisher_gid:
    93	            self.publisher_changes += 1
    94	            self.last_sequence = None
    95	        self.publisher_gid = gid
    96	        if self.last_sequence is not None:
    97	            if sequence > self.last_sequence:
    98	                self.missing_between_received += sequence - self.last_sequence - 1
    99	            else:
   100	                self.sequence_anomalies += 1
   101	        self.last_sequence = max(sequence, self.last_sequence) if self.last_sequence is not None else sequence
   102	        self.sequence_samples += 1
   103	
   104	    def observe_stamp(self, stamp_ns: int) -> None:
   105	        if self.last_stamp_ns is not None:
   106	            difference = stamp_ns - self.last_stamp_ns
   107	            if difference < 0:
   108	                self.stamp_regressions += 1
   109	            elif difference == 0:
   110	                self.repeated_stamps += 1
   111	            else:
   112	                self.max_capture_gap_ns = max(self.max_capture_gap_ns, difference)
   113	        self.last_stamp_ns = stamp_ns
   114	
   115	
   116	def read_cpu_ticks() -> dict:
   117	    """Read aggregate and logical CPU ticks, excluding duplicate guest fields."""
   118	    ticks = {}
   119	    for line in Path("/proc/stat").read_text().splitlines():
   120	        fields = line.split()
   121	        if fields and fields[0].startswith("cpu"):
   122	            values = [int(value) for value in fields[1:9]]
   123	            ticks[fields[0]] = (sum(values), values[3] + values[4])
   124	    return ticks
   125	
   126	
   127	def read_key_values(path: str) -> dict:
   128	    return {
   129	        fields[0].rstrip(":"): int(fields[1])
   130	        for line in Path(path).read_text().splitlines()
   131	        if len(fields := line.split()) >= 2
   132	    }
   133	
   134	
   135	def sample_hardware(previous) -> tuple:
   136	    """Sample host resources in a worker so nvidia-smi cannot block ROS callbacks."""
   137	    now_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
   138	    cpu = read_cpu_ticks()
   139	    memory = read_key_values("/proc/meminfo")
   140	    vm = read_key_values("/proc/vmstat")
   141	    rows = []
   142	    if previous is not None:
   143	        previous_ns, previous_cpu, previous_vm = previous
   144	        elapsed_s = (now_ns - previous_ns) / 1e9
   145	        busy = {}
   146	        for name, (total, idle) in cpu.items():
   147	            if name in previous_cpu:
   148	                old_total, old_idle = previous_cpu[name]
   149	                delta = total - old_total
   150	                if delta > 0:
   151	                    busy[name] = 100 * (delta - (idle - old_idle)) / delta
   152	        rows.append(("cpu_total_percent", f"{busy.get('cpu', 0.0):.1f}"))
   153	        logical = [value for name, value in busy.items() if name != "cpu"]
   154	        rows.append(("cpu_busiest_logical_percent", f"{max(logical, default=0.0):.1f}"))
   155	        page_mib = os.sysconf("SC_PAGE_SIZE") / (1024 * 1024)
   156	        for name, key in (("swap_in_mib_s", "pswpin"), ("swap_out_mib_s", "pswpout")):
   157	            rate = (vm[key] - previous_vm[key]) * page_mib / elapsed_s
   158	            rows.append((name, f"{rate:.3f}"))
   159	    else:
   160	        rows.extend((name, "warming_up") for name in (
   161	            "cpu_total_percent", "cpu_busiest_logical_percent",
   162	            "swap_in_mib_s", "swap_out_mib_s",
   163	        ))
   164	    rows.extend([
   165	        ("ram_total_mib", f"{memory['MemTotal'] / 1024:.1f}"),
   166	        ("ram_available_mib", f"{memory['MemAvailable'] / 1024:.1f}"),
   167	        ("swap_used_mib", f"{(memory['SwapTotal'] - memory['SwapFree']) / 1024:.1f}"),
   168	    ])
   169	    try:
   170	        result = subprocess.run(
   171	            ["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=index,utilization.gpu,memory.used,memory.total",
   172	             "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"],
   173	            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=2.0, check=False,
   174	        )
   175	    except FileNotFoundError:
   176	        rows.append(("gpu_status", "nvidia_smi_not_found"))
   177	    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   178	        rows.append(("gpu_status", "nvidia_smi_timeout"))
   179	    else:
   180	        rows.append(("gpu_status", "available" if result.returncode == 0 else "query_failed"))
   181	        if result.returncode:
   182	            rows.append(("gpu_query_error", " ".join(result.stderr.split())))
   183	        else:
   184	            for line in result.stdout.splitlines():
   185	                index, utilization, used, total = [field.strip() for field in line.split(",")]
   186	                rows.extend([
   187	                    (f"gpu{index}_util_percent", utilization),
   188	                    (f"gpu{index}_memory_used_mib", used),
   189	                    (f"gpu{index}_memory_total_mib", total),
   190	                ])
   191	    return (now_ns, cpu, vm), rows
   192	
   193	
   194	class TransportCheck(Node):
   195	    """Observe the existing three topics with the same best-effort queue depth."""
   196	
   197	    def __init__(self):
   198	        super().__init__("transport_check")
   199	        depth = self.declare_parameter("queue_depth", 5).value
   200	        if type(depth) is not int or depth <= 0:

   200	        if type(depth) is not int or depth <= 0:
   201	            raise ValueError("queue_depth must be a positive integer")
   202	        self.declare_parameter("stats_detail", False)
   203	        monitor_hardware = self.declare_parameter("monitor_hardware", True).value
   204	        self.streams = {name: StreamStats() for name in STREAMS}
   205	        self.clock_ns = None
   206	        self.clock_reset = False
   207	        self.last_clock_advance_ns = None
   208	        self.start_ns = self.previous_report_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
   209	        self.previous_clock_ns = None
   210	        self.previous_received = dict.fromkeys(STREAMS, 0)
   211	        self.pending = {}
   212	        self.closed_stamps = deque(maxlen=256)
   213	        self.complete_groups = 0
   214	        self.evicted_unmatched_groups = 0
   215	        self.blocked_after_reset = 0
   216	        self._hardware_previous = None
   217	        self._hardware_rows = [("hardware_status", "warming_up" if monitor_hardware else "disabled")]
   218	        self._hardware_pool = None
   219	        self._hardware_future = None
   220	        self.subscriptions_by_stream = {}
   221	        qos = QoSProfile(depth=depth, reliability=ReliabilityPolicy.BEST_EFFORT)
   222	        for stream, (topic, _encoding, _frame_id, _bytes) in STREAMS.items():
