   224	                subscription = self.create_subscription(
   225	                    Image, topic, partial(self._on_image, stream), qos,
   226	                    event_callbacks=SubscriptionEventCallbacks(
   227	                        message_lost=partial(self._on_lost, stream),
   228	                        matched=partial(self._on_matched, stream),
   229	                    ),
   230	                )
   231	            except UnsupportedEventTypeError:
   232	                self.streams[stream].dds_lost = None
   233	                self.streams[stream].matched_events_supported = False
   234	                self.get_logger().warning(
   235	                    f"Subscription events unsupported for {topic}; "
   236	                    "DDS loss and guarded sequence comparison are unknown"
   237	                )
   238	                subscription = self.create_subscription(
   239	                    Image, topic, partial(self._on_image, stream), qos
   240	                )
   241	            self.subscriptions_by_stream[stream] = subscription
   242	        self.clock_subscription = self.create_subscription(
   243	            Clock, "/clock", self._on_clock,
   244	            QoSProfile(depth=1, reliability=ReliabilityPolicy.BEST_EFFORT),
   245	        )
   246	        self._steady_clock = RclpyClock(clock_type=ClockType.STEADY_TIME)
   247	        self._timer = self.create_timer(1.0, self.report, clock=self._steady_clock)
   248	        if monitor_hardware:
   249	            self._hardware_pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1)
   250	            self._hardware_future = self._hardware_pool.submit(sample_hardware, None)
   251	        self.get_logger().info(
   252	            f"Host={socket.gethostname()}, PID={os.getpid()}, "
   253	            f"ROS_DOMAIN_ID={self.context.get_domain_id()}, "
   254	            f"RMW={rclpy.get_rmw_implementation_identifier()}, queue_depth={depth}. "
   255	            "Read-only diagnostic; no image copies or disk recording. "
   256	            "Sequence gaps exclude startup/tail loss and do not identify the loss layer. "
   257	            "Python publisher identity is guarded by graph data and matched events. "
   258	            "Hardware load is for this host; run here only if Isaac Sim runs here."
   259	        )
   260	
   261	    def _on_lost(self, stream: str, event) -> None:
   262	        self.streams[stream].dds_lost += event.total_count_change
   263	
   264	    def _on_matched(self, stream: str, event) -> None:
   265	        # A second match invalidates the entire interval, even after a disconnect.
   266	        stats = self.streams[stream]
   267	        stats.publisher_changes = max(stats.publisher_changes, event.total_count - 1)
   268	
   269	    def _on_clock(self, msg: Clock) -> None:
   270	        now_ns = msg.clock.sec * 1_000_000_000 + msg.clock.nanosec
   271	        if self.clock_ns is not None and now_ns < self.clock_ns and not self.clock_reset:
   272	            self.clock_reset = True
   273	            self.get_logger().error(
   274	                "Simulation clock moved backwards; image measurement stopped. "
   275	                "Restart this checker for the next run."
   276	            )
   277	        if self.clock_ns is None or now_ns > self.clock_ns:
   278	            self.last_clock_advance_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
   279	        self.clock_ns = now_ns
   280	
   281	    def _on_image(self, stream: str, msg: Image, info: dict) -> None:
   282	        if self.clock_reset:
   283	            self.blocked_after_reset += 1
   284	            return
   285	        stats = self.streams[stream]
   286	        stats.received += 1
   287	        _topic, encoding, frame_id, bytes_per_pixel = STREAMS[stream]
   288	        publishers = self.get_publishers_info_by_topic(_topic)
   289	        metadata = dict(info)
   290	        if len(publishers) == 1:
   291	            metadata.setdefault("publisher_gid", bytes(publishers[0].endpoint_gid))
   292	        else:
   293	            # Never compare sequences while identity is ambiguous or undiscovered.
   294	            metadata.pop("publisher_gid", None)
   295	            if len(publishers) > 1:
   296	                stats.publisher_changes = max(stats.publisher_changes, 1)
   297	        stats.observe_sequence(metadata)
   298	        if (
   299	            msg.encoding != encoding or msg.header.frame_id != frame_id
   300	            or msg.width == 0 or msg.height == 0
   301	            or msg.step < msg.width * bytes_per_pixel
   302	            or len(msg.data) != msg.height * msg.step
   303	            or msg.header.stamp.sec < 0
   304	            or not 0 <= msg.header.stamp.nanosec < 1_000_000_000
   305	        ):
   306	            stats.invalid += 1
   307	            self.get_logger().error(f"Invalid Image metadata or payload length on {stream}")
   308	            return
   309	        stamp_ns = msg.header.stamp.sec * 1_000_000_000 + msg.header.stamp.nanosec
   310	        stats.observe_stamp(stamp_ns)
   311	        # Retain only stamps and stream names. No timeout is inferred during Pause.
   312	        if stamp_ns in self.closed_stamps:
   313	            return
   314	        streams = self.pending.setdefault(stamp_ns, set())
   315	        streams.add(stream)
   316	        if len(streams) == len(STREAMS):
   317	            self.complete_groups += 1
   318	            del self.pending[stamp_ns]
   319	            self.closed_stamps.append(stamp_ns)
   320	        if len(self.pending) > 256:
   321	            del self.pending[next(iter(self.pending))]
   322	            self.evicted_unmatched_groups += 1
   323	
   324	    def report(self, *, final: bool = False) -> None:
   325	        """Emit one block; wall rates and observed simulation progress stay separate."""
   326	        show_details = self.get_parameter("stats_detail").value
   327	        now_ns = time.monotonic_ns()
   328	        wall_s = (now_ns - self.previous_report_ns) / 1e9
   329	        ratio = "unknown"
   330	        if self.previous_clock_ns is not None and not self.clock_reset:
   331	            ratio = f"{(self.clock_ns - self.previous_clock_ns) / 1e9 / wall_s:.3f}"
   332	        counts = {
   333	            name: subscription.get_publisher_count()
   334	            for name, subscription in self.subscriptions_by_stream.items()
   335	        }
   336	        clock_publishers = self.clock_subscription.get_publisher_count()
   337	        ready = all(count == 1 for count in counts.values()) and clock_publishers == 1
   338	        if self._hardware_future is not None and self._hardware_future.done():
   339	            # Unexpected sampling errors propagate with their original traceback.
   340	            self._hardware_previous, self._hardware_rows = self._hardware_future.result()
   341	            self._hardware_future = None
   342	        hardware_age = (
   343	            "unknown" if self._hardware_previous is None
   344	            else f"{(now_ns - self._hardware_previous[0]) / 1e9:.3f}"
   345	        )
   346	        rows = [
   347	            ("report_type", "final" if final else "periodic"),
   348	            ("elapsed_wall_s", f"{(now_ns - self.start_ns) / 1e9:.3f}"),
   349	            ("window_wall_s", f"{wall_s:.3f}"),
   350	            ("publishers_ready", ready),
   351	            ("clock_publishers", clock_publishers),
   352	            ("sim_wall_ratio_est", ratio),
   353	            ("clock_idle_wall_s", "unknown" if self.last_clock_advance_ns is None
   354	             else f"{(now_ns - self.last_clock_advance_ns) / 1e9:.3f}"),
   355	            ("clock_reset", self.clock_reset),
   356	            ("blocked_after_reset", self.blocked_after_reset),
   357	            None,
   358	            ("complete_groups_total", self.complete_groups),
   359	            ("unmatched_observed_groups", len(self.pending)),
   360	            ("evicted_unmatched_groups", self.evicted_unmatched_groups),
   361	        ]
   362	        for name, stats in self.streams.items():
   363	            rate = (stats.received - self.previous_received[name]) / wall_s
   364	            missing = stats.missing_between_received if stats.sequence_status == "available" else "unknown"
   365	            rows.extend([
   366	                None,
   367	                (f"{name}_publishers", counts[name]),
   368	                (f"{name}_received_total", stats.received),
   369	                (f"{name}_receive_hz_wall", f"{rate:.3f}"),
   370	                (f"{name}_seq_status", stats.sequence_status),
   371	                (f"{name}_seq_missing_total", missing),
   372	                (f"{name}_dds_lost_total", "unknown" if stats.dds_lost is None else stats.dds_lost),
   373	                (f"{name}_invalid_total", stats.invalid),
   374	            ])
   375	            if show_details:
   376	                rows.extend([
   377	                    (f"{name}_last_stamp_ns", stats.last_stamp_ns),
   378	                    (f"{name}_capture_gap_max_ms", f"{stats.max_capture_gap_ns / 1e6:.3f}"),
   379	                    (f"{name}_stamp_regressions", stats.stamp_regressions),
   380	                    (f"{name}_repeated_stamps", stats.repeated_stamps),
   381	                    (f"{name}_publisher_changes", stats.publisher_changes),
   382	                    (f"{name}_sequence_anomalies", stats.sequence_anomalies),
   383	                ])
   384	            self.previous_received[name] = stats.received
   385	        rows.extend([None, ("hardware_sample_age_s", hardware_age), *self._hardware_rows])
   386	        if not show_details:
   387	            summary_fields = {
   388	                "publishers_ready", "sim_wall_ratio_est", "complete_groups_total",
   389	                "fpv_rgb_seq_missing_total", "fpv_depth_seq_missing_total",
   390	                "top_rgb_seq_missing_total", "cpu_busiest_logical_percent",
   391	                "ram_available_mib", "hardware_status", "gpu_query_error",
   392	            }
   393	            # Keep raw per-stream loss counts; presentation does not change counters.
   394	            rows = [
   395	                row for row in rows
   396	                if row is not None and (
   397	                    row[0] in summary_fields
   398	                    or row[0].startswith("gpu") and row[0].endswith(
   399	                        ("_util_percent", "_memory_used_mib")
   400	                    )
   401	                    or row[0] == "gpu_status" and row[1] != "available"
   402	                    or row[0] == "clock_reset" and row[1]
   403	                )
   404	            ]
   405	        lines = ["=" * 64]
   406	        lines.extend("" if row is None else f"{row[0]:<36} : {row[1]}" for row in rows)
   407	        lines.append("=" * 64)
   408	        self.get_logger().info("\n" + "\n".join(lines))
   409	        self.previous_report_ns = now_ns
   410	        self.previous_clock_ns = self.clock_ns
   411	        if self._hardware_pool is not None and self._hardware_future is None and not final:
   412	            self._hardware_future = self._hardware_pool.submit(sample_hardware, self._hardware_previous)
   413	
   414	    def close_hardware(self) -> None:
   415	        if self._hardware_pool is not None:
   416	            self._hardware_pool.shutdown(wait=True, cancel_futures=True)
   417	
   418	
   419	def main(args=None) -> None:
   420	    # Preserve Python's KeyboardInterrupt so a final report can precede shutdown.
   421	    rclpy.init(args=args, signal_handler_options=SignalHandlerOptions.NO)
   422	    node = None
   423	    try:
   424	        node = TransportCheck()
   425	        rclpy.spin(node)
   426	    except (KeyboardInterrupt, ExternalShutdownException):
   427	        pass
   428	    finally:
   429	        if node is not None:
   430	            try:
   431	                if rclpy.ok():
   432	                    node.report(final=True)
   433	            finally:
   434	                node.close_hardware()
   435	                node.destroy_node()
   436	        if rclpy.ok():
   437	            rclpy.shutdown()
   438	
   439	
   440	if __name__ == "__main__":
   441	    main()

async def initialize_pegasus(scene_path):
    pg = PegasusInterface()
    if pg.get_all_vehicles():
        print("Pegasus vehicle already active; initialization skipped")
        return

    print(f"Loading scene: {scene_path}")
    stage_opened, error = await open_stage_async(scene_path)
    if not stage_opened:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to open scene: {error}")
        
    pg.initialize_world()
    await pg.world.initialize_simulation_context_async()
    print("Pegasus World ready")
    stage = pg.world.stage
    if stage.GetPrimAtPath("/World/quadrotor"):
        stage.RemovePrim("/World/quadrotor")

    mavlink_config = PX4MavlinkBackendConfig({
        "vehicle_id": 0,

    """

    → 確認兩者 stamp 與 render_reference 相同
    → 距上次發布至少經過 200 ms 模擬時間

    camera_profile_stages = ("read", "resize", "pack", "publish_call")
    camera_cpu_profile = {}

    def reset_camera_profile():
        """Start the next timing window at the first active camera callback."""
        camera_cpu_profile.update(
            window_start_ns=None,
            cpu_ns=0,
            wall_ns=0,
            wall_max_ns=0,
            calls=0,
            published_groups=0,
        )
        for stage_name in camera_profile_stages:
            camera_cpu_profile[f"{stage_name}_cpu_ns"] = 0
            camera_cpu_profile[f"{stage_name}_wall_ns"] = 0

    reset_camera_profile()

    def reset_camera_readers(event):
        nonlocal next_camera_log_time, last_camera_publish_stamp_ns
        nonlocal camera_published_groups
        if event.type in (
            int(omni.timeline.TimelineEventType.PLAY),
            int(omni.timeline.TimelineEventType.PAUSE),
            int(omni.timeline.TimelineEventType.STOP),
        ):
            reset_camera_profile()
            next_camera_log_time = 0.0
        if event.type == int(omni.timeline.TimelineEventType.STOP):
            for reader in camera_readers.values():
    def read_cameras(_event):
        nonlocal next_camera_log_time, last_camera_publish_stamp_ns
        nonlocal camera_published_groups
        if not pg.world.is_playing():
            return

        camera_work_wall_start_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
        if camera_cpu_profile["window_start_ns"] is None:
            camera_cpu_profile["window_start_ns"] = camera_work_wall_start_ns
            next_camera_log_time = time.monotonic() + 1.0
        camera_work_start_ns = time.thread_time_ns()

        try:
            read_wall_start_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
            read_cpu_start_ns = time.thread_time_ns()
            for reader in camera_readers.values():
                # Reader 等待影像或讀到重複 frame 時，不會重複發布
                reader.read()
            camera_cpu_profile["read_cpu_ns"] += (
                time.thread_time_ns() - read_cpu_start_ns
            )
            camera_cpu_profile["read_wall_ns"] += (
                time.perf_counter_ns() - read_wall_start_ns
            )

            fpv_frame = camera_readers["fpv_camera"].last_frame
            top_frame = camera_readers["top_camera"].last_frame
            readers_ready = all(
                reader.status in ("new_frame", "repeated_frame")
                for reader in camera_readers.values()
            )
            frames_aligned = (
                readers_ready
                and fpv_frame is not None
                and top_frame is not None
                and fpv_frame["stamp_ns"] == top_frame["stamp_ns"]
                and fpv_frame["render_reference"] == top_frame["render_reference"]
            )
            if frames_aligned:
                stamp_ns = fpv_frame["stamp_ns"]
                interval_ready = (
                    last_camera_publish_stamp_ns is None
                    or stamp_ns - last_camera_publish_stamp_ns >= camera_publish_period_ns # 核心判斷
                )
                if interval_ready:
                    for name, frame in (
                        ("fpv_camera", fpv_frame),
                        ("top_camera", top_frame),
                    ):
                        publisher = camera_publishers[name]
                        publisher.publish(frame)
                        for key, value in publisher.last_profile.items():
                            camera_cpu_profile[key] += value
                    last_camera_publish_stamp_ns = stamp_ns
                    # Count completed publish calls, not subscriber deliveries.
                    camera_published_groups += 1
                    camera_cpu_profile["published_groups"] += 1
        except Exception:
            pg.world.remove_render_callback("read_cameras")
            sim_node.get_logger().error(
                "Camera reading and publishing stopped after an error; see traceback"
            )
            raise
        # 每次執行都會累計，包含沒有達到發布間隔的 callback
        camera_cpu_profile["cpu_ns"] += (
            time.thread_time_ns() - camera_work_start_ns
        )
        camera_work_wall_end_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
        camera_work_wall_ns = camera_work_wall_end_ns - camera_work_wall_start_ns
        camera_cpu_profile["wall_ns"] += camera_work_wall_ns
        camera_cpu_profile["wall_max_ns"] = max(
            camera_cpu_profile["wall_max_ns"], camera_work_wall_ns
        )
        camera_cpu_profile["calls"] += 1
        # Wall time only limits console output; capture stamps come from snapshots.
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now < next_camera_log_time:
            return
        next_camera_log_time = now + 1.0
        profile_now_ns = camera_work_wall_end_ns
        window_ns = profile_now_ns - camera_cpu_profile["window_start_ns"]
        cpu_ms = camera_cpu_profile["cpu_ns"] / 1_000_000
        cpu_percent = 100 * camera_cpu_profile["cpu_ns"] / window_ns
        wall_ms = camera_cpu_profile["wall_ns"] / 1_000_000
        wall_max_ms = camera_cpu_profile["wall_max_ns"] / 1_000_000
        show_details = sim_node.get_parameter("stats_detail").value

        rows = [
            ("camera_callback_thread_id", threading.get_native_id()),
            ("profile_window_wall_s", f"{window_ns / 1e9:.3f}"),
            ("camera_calls", camera_cpu_profile["calls"]),
            ("published_groups_window", camera_cpu_profile["published_groups"]),
            ("published_groups_total", camera_published_groups),
            None,
            ("camera_work_cpu_percent", f"{cpu_percent:.3f}"),
            ("camera_work_cpu_percent", f"{cpu_percent:.3f}"),
            ("camera_work_wall_max_ms", f"{wall_max_ms:.3f}"),
        ]
        if show_details:
            rows.extend([
                ("latest_published_stamp_ns", last_camera_publish_stamp_ns),
                ("camera_work_cpu_ms", f"{cpu_ms:.3f}"),
                ("camera_work_wall_ms", f"{wall_ms:.3f}"),
            ])
        rows.append(None)
        for stage_name in camera_profile_stages:
            stage_cpu_ms = camera_cpu_profile[f"{stage_name}_cpu_ns"] / 1_000_000
            rows.append((f"{stage_name}_cpu_ms", f"{stage_cpu_ms:.3f}"))
            if show_details:
                stage_wall_ms = camera_cpu_profile[f"{stage_name}_wall_ns"] / 1_000_000
                rows.append((f"{stage_name}_wall_ms", f"{stage_wall_ms:.3f}"))
        rows.append(None)

        # Keep the source diagnostics in the same report, outside the work timer.
        if show_details:
            for name, reader in camera_readers.items():
                prefix = "fpv" if name == "fpv_camera" else "top"
                rows.extend([
                    (f"{prefix}_status", reader.status),
                    (f"{prefix}_new_frames", reader.new_frames),
                    (f"{prefix}_repeated_reads", reader.repeated_reads),
                ])
                if reader.status == "waiting_for_render":
                    rows.append((f"{prefix}_wait_detail", reader.wait_detail))
                frame = reader.last_frame
                if frame is not None:
                    rows.extend([
                        (f"{prefix}_capture_time_s", f"{frame['rendering_time']:.6f}"),
                        (f"{prefix}_stamp_ns", frame["stamp_ns"]),
                        (f"{prefix}_dt_ns", frame["dt_ns"]),
                        (f"{prefix}_render_reference", frame["render_reference"]),
                        (f"{prefix}_rgb_shape", frame["rgb"].shape),
                        (f"{prefix}_rgb_dtype", frame["rgb"].dtype),
                    ])
                    depth = frame["depth"]
                    if depth is not None:
                        valid = np.isfinite(depth) & (depth > 0)
                        valid_ratio = np.count_nonzero(valid) / depth.size
                        rows.extend([
                            (f"{prefix}_depth_shape", depth.shape),
                            (f"{prefix}_depth_dtype", depth.dtype),
                            (f"{prefix}_depth_valid_ratio", f"{valid_ratio:.3f}"),
                        ])
                rows.append(None)
            rows.append(("min_publish_interval_ns", camera_publish_period_ns))
        if readers_ready and fpv_frame is not None and top_frame is not None:
            delta_ns = top_frame["stamp_ns"] - fpv_frame["stamp_ns"]
            same_reference = top_frame["render_reference"] == fpv_frame["render_reference"]
            rows.extend([
                ("latest_stamp_delta_ns", delta_ns),
                ("same_render_reference", same_reference),

    def _timed_out(self):
        self.future.set_result(True)

    def _subscriber_callback(self, msg, info):
        submsg = msg
        if self.field is not None:
            for field in self.field:
                try:
                    submsg = getattr(submsg, field)
                except AttributeError as ex:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Invalid field '{'.'.join(self.field)}': {ex}")

        # Evaluate the current msg against the supplied expression
        if self.filter_fn is not None and not self.filter_fn(submsg):
            return

        if self.future is not None and self.once:
            self.future.set_result(True)

        if not hasattr(submsg, '__slots__'):
            # raw
            if self.include_message_info:
                print('---Got new message, message info:---')
                print(info)
                print('---Message data:---')
            print(submsg, end='\n---\n')
            return

        if self.csv:

    79	                f"shape={color.shape}, dtype={color.dtype}"
    80	            )
    81	        rgb = np.ascontiguousarray(color[:, :, :3])
    82	
    83	        if self.include_depth:
    84	            if depth.ndim == 3 and depth.shape[2] == 1:
    85	                depth = depth[:, :, 0]
    86	            if depth.shape != expected_shape or depth.dtype != np.float32:
    87	                raise ValueError(
    88	                    f"{sensor_state['camera_name']}: unexpected depth array "
    89	                    f"shape={depth.shape}, dtype={depth.dtype}"
    90	                )
    91	
    92	        rendering_time = float(snapshot["rendering_time"])
    93	        if not np.isfinite(rendering_time) or rendering_time < 0:
    94	            raise ValueError(f"Invalid rendering time: {rendering_time}")
    95	        stamp_ns = round(rendering_time * 1_000_000_000)
    96	        previous_stamp = None if self.last_frame is None else self.last_frame["stamp_ns"]
    97	        if previous_stamp is not None and stamp_ns <= previous_stamp:
    98	            raise ValueError(
    99	                f"{sensor_state['camera_name']}: capture time did not advance "
   100	                f"({previous_stamp} -> {stamp_ns}); reset reader state before a new run"
   101	            )
   102	
   103	        reference = snapshot["rendering_frame"]
   104	        # read() 的輸出
   105	        self.last_frame = {
   106	            "camera_name": sensor_state["camera_name"],
   107	            "camera_prim_path": sensor_state["stage_prim_path"],
   108	            "rendering_time": rendering_time,
   109	            "stamp_ns": stamp_ns,
   110	            "dt_ns": None if previous_stamp is None else stamp_ns - previous_stamp,
   111	            "render_reference": (
   112	                int(reference["referenceTimeNumerator"]),
   113	                int(reference["referenceTimeDenominator"]),
   114	            ),
   115	            "rgb": rgb,
   116	            "depth": depth if self.include_depth else None,
   117	        }
   118	        self.new_frames += 1
   119	        self.status = "new_frame"
   211	        channels = pixels.shape[2] if pixels.ndim == 3 else 1
   212	        msg = Image()
   213	        msg.header.stamp.sec, msg.header.stamp.nanosec = divmod(
   214	            stamp_ns, 1_000_000_000
   215	        )
   216	        msg.header.frame_id = self.frame_id
   217	        msg.height = int(height)
   218	        msg.width = int(width)
   219	        msg.encoding = encoding
   220	        msg.is_bigendian = int(sys.byteorder == "big")
   221	        msg.step = int(width * channels * pixels.dtype.itemsize)
   222	        # C order packs each row; array('B') avoids per-byte Python validation.
   223	        msg.data = array("B", pixels.tobytes(order="C"))
   224	        return msg
   225	
   226	    # rgb8 是每個色彩通道 8 bits；32FC1 是每個像素一個 32-bit 浮點數。
   227	    def publish(self, frame) -> None:
   228	        """Publish one new snapshot, preserving a shared RGB/depth timestamp."""
   229	        if self.depth_publisher is not None and frame["depth"] is None:
   230	            raise ValueError("Depth publisher requires a snapshot with depth")
   231	
   232	        # Measure only this calling thread; source acquisition stamps stay intact.
   233	        profile = {}
   234	        wall_start_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
   235	        cpu_start_ns = time.thread_time_ns()
   236	        rgb = self._resize_pixels(frame["rgb"], PILImage.Resampling.BOX)
   237	        depth = None
   238	        if self.depth_publisher is not None:
   239	            # Nearest-neighbor sampling keeps source depth values in meters.
   240	            depth = self._resize_pixels(
   241	                frame["depth"], PILImage.Resampling.NEAREST
   242	            )
   243	        profile["resize_cpu_ns"] = time.thread_time_ns() - cpu_start_ns
   244	        profile["resize_wall_ns"] = time.perf_counter_ns() - wall_start_ns
   245	
   246	        wall_start_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
   247	        cpu_start_ns = time.thread_time_ns()
   248	        rgb_msg = self._make_image(rgb, "rgb8", frame["stamp_ns"])
   249	        depth_msg = None
   250	        if depth is not None:
   251	            depth_msg = self._make_image(depth, "32FC1", frame["stamp_ns"])
   252	        profile["pack_cpu_ns"] = time.thread_time_ns() - cpu_start_ns
   253	        profile["pack_wall_ns"] = time.perf_counter_ns() - wall_start_ns
   254	
   255	        wall_start_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
   256	        cpu_start_ns = time.thread_time_ns()
   257	        self.rgb_publisher.publish(rgb_msg)
   258	        if depth_msg is not None:
   259	            self.depth_publisher.publish(depth_msg)
   260	        # Returning from publish() does not acknowledge subscriber delivery.
   261	        profile["publish_call_cpu_ns"] = time.thread_time_ns() - cpu_start_ns
   262	        profile["publish_call_wall_ns"] = time.perf_counter_ns() - wall_start_ns
   263	        self.last_profile = profile

   153	    def reset_camera_profile():
   154	        """Start the next timing window at the first active camera callback."""
   155	        camera_cpu_profile.update(
   156	            window_start_ns=None,
   157	            cpu_ns=0,

   166	
   191	    def read_cameras(_event):
   192	        nonlocal next_camera_log_time, last_camera_publish_stamp_ns
   193	        nonlocal camera_published_groups
   194	        if not pg.world.is_playing():
   195	            return
   196	
   197	        camera_work_wall_start_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
   198	        if camera_cpu_profile["window_start_ns"] is None:
   199	            camera_cpu_profile["window_start_ns"] = camera_work_wall_start_ns
   200	            next_camera_log_time = time.monotonic() + 1.0
   201	        camera_work_start_ns = time.thread_time_ns()
   202	
   203	        try:
   204	            read_wall_start_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
   205	            read_cpu_start_ns = time.thread_time_ns()
   206	            for reader in camera_readers.values():
   207	                # Reader 等待影像或讀到重複 frame 時，不會重複發布
   208	                reader.read()
   209	            camera_cpu_profile["read_cpu_ns"] += (
   210	                time.thread_time_ns() - read_cpu_start_ns
   211	            )
   212	            camera_cpu_profile["read_wall_ns"] += (
   213	                time.perf_counter_ns() - read_wall_start_ns
   214	            )
   215	
   216	            fpv_frame = camera_readers["fpv_camera"].last_frame
   217	            top_frame = camera_readers["top_camera"].last_frame
   218	            readers_ready = all(
   219	                reader.status in ("new_frame", "repeated_frame")
   220	                for reader in camera_readers.values()
   221	            )
   222	            frames_aligned = (
   223	                readers_ready
   224	                and fpv_frame is not None
   225	                and top_frame is not None
   226	                and fpv_frame["stamp_ns"] == top_frame["stamp_ns"]
   227	                and fpv_frame["render_reference"] == top_frame["render_reference"]
   228	            )
   229	            if frames_aligned:
   230	                stamp_ns = fpv_frame["stamp_ns"]
   231	                interval_ready = (
   232	                    last_camera_publish_stamp_ns is None
   233	                    or stamp_ns - last_camera_publish_stamp_ns >= camera_publish_period_ns # 核心判斷
   234	                )
   235	                if interval_ready:
   236	                    for name, frame in (
   237	                        ("fpv_camera", fpv_frame),
   238	                        ("top_camera", top_frame),
   239	                    ):
   240	                        publisher = camera_publishers[name]
   241	                        publisher.publish(frame)
   242	                        for key, value in publisher.last_profile.items():
   243	                            camera_cpu_profile[key] += value
   244	                    last_camera_publish_stamp_ns = stamp_ns
   245	                    # Count completed publish calls, not subscriber deliveries.
   246	                    camera_published_groups += 1
   247	                    camera_cpu_profile["published_groups"] += 1
   248	        except Exception:
   249	            pg.world.remove_render_callback("read_cameras")
   250	            sim_node.get_logger().error(
   251	                "Camera reading and publishing stopped after an error; see traceback"
   252	            )
   253	            raise
   254	        # 每次執行都會累計，包含沒有達到發布間隔的 callback
   255	        camera_cpu_profile["cpu_ns"] += (
   256	            time.thread_time_ns() - camera_work_start_ns
   257	        )
   258	        camera_work_wall_end_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
   259	        camera_work_wall_ns = camera_work_wall_end_ns - camera_work_wall_start_ns
   260	        camera_cpu_profile["wall_ns"] += camera_work_wall_ns
   261	        camera_cpu_profile["wall_max_ns"] = max(
   262	            camera_cpu_profile["wall_max_ns"], camera_work_wall_ns
   263	        )
   264	        camera_cpu_profile["calls"] += 1
   265	        # Wall time only limits console output; capture stamps come from snapshots.
   266	        now = time.monotonic()
   267	        if now < next_camera_log_time:
   268	            return
   269	        next_camera_log_time = now + 1.0
   270	        profile_now_ns = camera_work_wall_end_ns
   271	        window_ns = profile_now_ns - camera_cpu_profile["window_start_ns"]
   272	        cpu_ms = camera_cpu_profile["cpu_ns"] / 1_000_000
   273	        cpu_percent = 100 * camera_cpu_profile["cpu_ns"] / window_ns
   274	        wall_ms = camera_cpu_profile["wall_ns"] / 1_000_000
   275	        wall_max_ms = camera_cpu_profile["wall_max_ns"] / 1_000_000
   276	        show_details = sim_node.get_parameter("stats_detail").value
   278	        rows = [
   279	            ("camera_callback_thread_id", threading.get_native_id()),
   280	            ("profile_window_wall_s", f"{window_ns / 1e9:.3f}"),
   281	            ("camera_calls", camera_cpu_profile["calls"]),
   282	            ("published_groups_window", camera_cpu_profile["published_groups"]),
   283	            ("published_groups_total", camera_published_groups),
   284	            None,
   285	            ("camera_work_cpu_percent", f"{cpu_percent:.3f}"),
   286	            ("camera_work_wall_max_ms", f"{wall_max_ms:.3f}"),
   287	        ]
   288	        if show_details:
   289	            rows.extend([
   290	                ("latest_published_stamp_ns", last_camera_publish_stamp_ns),
   291	                ("camera_work_cpu_ms", f"{cpu_ms:.3f}"),
   292	                ("camera_work_wall_ms", f"{wall_ms:.3f}"),
   293	            ])
   294	        rows.append(None)
   295	        for stage_name in camera_profile_stages:
   296	            stage_cpu_ms = camera_cpu_profile[f"{stage_name}_cpu_ns"] / 1_000_000
   297	            rows.append((f"{stage_name}_cpu_ms", f"{stage_cpu_ms:.3f}"))
   298	            if show_details:
   299	                stage_wall_ms = camera_cpu_profile[f"{stage_name}_wall_ns"] / 1_000_000
   300	                rows.append((f"{stage_name}_wall_ms", f"{stage_wall_ms:.3f}"))
   301	        rows.append(None)
   302	
   330	                            (f"{prefix}_depth_dtype", depth.dtype),
   331	                            (f"{prefix}_depth_valid_ratio", f"{valid_ratio:.3f}"),
